afraid of something = 害怕某物
proud of something = 为某事自豪
⑨ The arrival of the train was announced twenty minutes late.
= 火车进站的广播晚了二十分钟。
the arrival of the train = 火车进站
⑩ She is a woman of great courage and very few words.
= 她是个极有勇气、话很少的女人。
a woman of courage = 有勇气的女人
⑪ The shop is closed on the first Monday of every month.
= 那家店每月的头一个星期一不开门。
the first Monday of the month = 每月头一个星期一
⑫ The village lies ten miles north of the river.
= 那座村子在河以北十英里。
north of something = 在某物以北
⑬ It was kind of you to wait for me in the rain.
= 你冒雨等我，真是太好了。
it is kind of you to do something = 你这样做真好
⑭ "May I borrow your pen?" "Of course."
= 「我能借一下你的笔吗？」「当然。」
of course = 当然
⑮ And who should win the prize, of all people, but my own brother.
= 而得奖的偏偏不是别人，正是我的亲兄弟。
of all people = 偏偏是这个人
of all things = 偏偏是这样东西
⑯ She has been very quiet of late and hardly leaves the house.
= 她近来很沉默，几乎不出门。
of late = 近来

off
/ɒf/
核心：副词、介词、形容词都用，底子是「离开、脱离、断开」，是 on 的反义。
一是离开、走开：be off 是要走了，set off 是出发；也指距离与时间上「隔着」：two miles off、a long way off。
二是从…上下来、脱离：fall off a ladder、take your coat off；三是（电器、水、电）关着：turn the light off；
四是不上班、休假：a day off、off duty；五是取消了：the match is off；六是减价：ten per cent off；
七是（食物）变质了：the milk is off；八是从…岔出去、靠近：a street off the square；
九是不再喜欢、戒了：be off your food。
形容词：不太对劲、不如平时的：an off day；off colour 是气色不好。
习语：off and on（或 on and off）是断断续续；on the off chance 是抱着万一的希望；
well off 是富裕，badly off 是穷，better off 是境况更好、还不如；off the record 是不供发表、私下说。
以它打头的复合词并入本条：off-air（不在播出时）、off-centre（偏离中心）、off-grid 与 off-the-grid（不接公共水电网的）、
off-key（走调）、off-kilter（歪的、不对劲）、off-label（药物超说明书使用）、off-licence（英式，卖酒的外卖店）、
off-limits（禁止入内的、不许碰的）、off-peak（非高峰的）、off-piste（雪道以外的）、off-putting（让人反感的）、
off-ramp（美式，高速公路出口匝道）、off-road（越野的；off-roader 是越野车，off-roading 是越野驾驶）、off-sale（外卖酒类）、
off-screen（银幕之外）、off season（淡季）、off-street（不在街边的）、off-the-shelf（现成的）、off-white（米白色）、
off year（没有大选的年份、成绩差的一年）。
① I must be off, or I shall miss the last bus.
= 我得走了，不然要误末班车。
be off = 要走了
② We set off at dawn and reached the coast by noon.
= 我们黎明出发，中午到了海边。
set off = 出发
③ The nearest garage is two miles off, on the far side of the hill.
= 最近的修车铺在两英里外，小山的那一边。
two miles off = 在两英里外
④ The exams are still a long way off, so there is time to catch up.
= 考试还早着呢，来得及赶上去。
a long way off = 还很远
⑤ He fell off the ladder while painting the upstairs windows.
= 他漆楼上的窗户时从梯子上摔了下来。
fall off something = 从某物上摔下来
⑥ Take your coat off and sit by the fire.
= 把大衣脱了，到炉边坐坐。
take something off = 脱下某物
⑦ Please turn the lights off when you leave the room.
= 离开房间时请关灯。
turn something off = 关掉某物
⑧ She has taken a day off to wait for the plumber.
= 她请了一天假，在家等水管工。
a day off = 一天假
⑨ The nurse was off duty when the call came.
= 电话打来的时候，那位护士不当班。
off duty = 不当班
⑩ The wedding is off, and nobody will say why.
= 婚礼取消了，谁也不肯说为什么。
be off = 取消了
⑪ The shop is offering twenty per cent off all winter coats.
= 那家店所有冬大衣打八折。
twenty per cent off = 减价百分之二十
⑫ This milk is off, so pour it down the sink.
= 这牛奶坏了，倒进水槽吧。
be off = 变质了
⑬ The hotel is in a quiet street off the main square.
= 那家旅馆在主广场旁边的一条僻静小街上。
a street off the square = 广场旁岔出去的街
⑭ He has been off his food since the operation.
= 手术以后，他一直没胃口。
be off your food = 没胃口
⑮ Even the best players have an off day now and then.
= 再好的球员偶尔也有状态不佳的一天。
an off day = 状态不佳的一天
⑯ She was feeling a bit off colour and went home at lunchtime.
= 她觉得有点不舒服，午饭时就回家了。
off colour = 气色不好、不舒服
⑰ It rained off and on for the whole of the holiday.
= 整个假期，雨断断续续地下。
off and on = 断断续续
⑱ I rang on the off chance that somebody had handed in the keys.
= 我抱着万一的希望打了个电话，看有没有人把钥匙交上来。
on the off chance = 抱着万一的希望
⑲ They are not well off, but they manage a holiday every summer.
= 他们并不富裕，但每年夏天都设法出去度个假。
well off = 富裕
badly off = 穷
⑳ You would be better off taking the train than sitting in that traffic.
= 与其堵在那车流里，你还不如坐火车。
better off = 境况更好、还不如
㉑ The minister agreed to speak only off the record.
= 那位大臣只肯私下谈，不供发表。
off the record = 不供发表、私下说
㉒ The two presenters are good friends off-air, whatever they say in the studio.
= 不管在演播室里怎么说，那两位主持人下了节目是好朋友。
off-air = 不在播出时
㉓ The picture on the wall is slightly off-centre and annoys everyone.
= 墙上那幅画挂得稍微有点偏，人人看着别扭。
off-centre = 偏离中心
㉔ They live off-grid in a cabin with solar panels and a well.
= 他们住在一间不接公共水电的小木屋里，靠太阳能板和一口井。
off-grid = 不接公共水电网的
off-the-grid = 不接公共水电网的
㉕ The choir sang slightly off-key in the cold church.
= 在冰冷的教堂里，唱诗班唱得有点走调。
off-key = 走调
㉖ The shelf is off-kilter, and the books slide to one end.
= 架子是歪的，书都滑到了一头。
off-kilter = 歪的、不对劲
㉗ The drug is often prescribed off-label for patients who cannot sleep.
= 那种药常被超说明书开给睡不着的病人。
off-label = 超说明书使用
㉘ He stopped at the off-licence on the corner for a bottle of wine.
= 他在街角那家卖酒的店停了一下，买了瓶葡萄酒。
an off-licence = 卖酒的外卖店
㉙ The upper floor is off-limits to visitors.
= 楼上谢绝访客。
off-limits = 禁止入内的
㉚ Tickets are half the price if you travel at off-peak times.
= 你要是在非高峰时段出行，票价只要一半。
off-peak = 非高峰的
㉛ Two skiers were caught by an avalanche while skiing off-piste.
= 两名滑雪者在雪道以外滑雪时遇上了雪崩。
off-piste = 雪道以外的
㉜ I found his habit of interrupting very off-putting.
= 他爱打断别人的习惯，我觉得很让人反感。
off-putting = 让人反感的
㉝ Take the second off-ramp after the bridge and turn left at the lights.
= 过桥后从第二个出口匝道下去，到红绿灯左拐。
an off-ramp = 出口匝道
㉞ The track to the farm needs an off-road vehicle in winter.
= 冬天去那座农场的土路，得开越野车。
off-road = 越野的
㉟ He drives a huge off-roader that has never left the tarmac.
= 他开一辆巨大的越野车，却从没离开过柏油路。
an off-roader = 越野车
㊱ They spend their weekends off-roading in the hills above the town.
= 他们周末在镇子上方的山里玩越野驾驶。
off-roading = 越野驾驶
㊲ The pub has a licence for off-sales, so you can take a bottle home.
= 那家酒馆有外卖酒类的执照，你可以带一瓶回家。
off-sale = 外卖酒类
㊳ The two actors play enemies in the film but are close friends off-screen.
= 那两位演员在片子里演仇人，银幕之外却是好朋友。
off-screen = 银幕之外
㊴ Hotel rooms on the island cost half as much in the off season.
= 岛上的旅馆房间，淡季只要一半的价钱。
the off season = 淡季
㊵ The flat comes with off-street parking for one car.
= 那套公寓带一个不占街边的停车位。
off-street parking = 不占街边的停车位
㊶ The firm bought an off-the-shelf system instead of writing its own.
= 那家公司买了一套现成的系统，没有自己编写。
off-the-shelf = 现成的
㊷ The walls are painted off-white, with a darker shade round the windows.
= 墙刷的是米白色，窗户四周颜色深一些。
off-white = 米白色
㊸ Voters often stay at home in an off year, when only local seats are at stake.
= 在没有大选、只选地方席位的年份，选民往往待在家里不去投票。
an off year = 没有大选的年份

offal
/ˈɒfəl/
核心：下水、内脏：动物的心、肝、肾等供食用的部分。不可数，英式说法，美式多说 variety meats。
① The butcher sells offal on Thursdays, and the liver goes within an hour.
= 那家肉铺星期四卖下水，肝不到一个钟头就卖光。
offal = 下水
② Her grandmother could turn the cheapest offal into a stew worth queuing for.
= 她祖母能把最便宜的下水做成一锅值得排队的炖菜。
cheap offal = 便宜的下水

offbeat
/ˌɒfˈbiːt/
核心：不落俗套的、另类的：跟大多数人想的、做的不一样，多含褒义。形容词，口语。
① The café is known for its offbeat menu, which includes beetroot ice cream.
= 那家咖啡馆以不落俗套的菜单出名，上面有甜菜根冰激凌。
an offbeat menu = 不落俗套的菜单
② She has an offbeat sense of humour that takes a while to appreciate.
= 她的幽默感很另类，要过一阵子才品得出来。
an offbeat sense of humour = 另类的幽默感

offcut
/ˈɒfkʌt/
核心：边角料、下脚料：从整块木头、布料、地毯、纸上裁下来的剩余小块。可数，英式。
① He built the bird table from offcuts of timber left by the builders.
= 那个喂鸟台是他用建筑工人剩下的木料边角做的。
offcuts of timber = 木料边角
② The carpet shop sells offcuts cheaply for use in cars and caravans.
= 那家地毯店便宜出售边角料，供人铺在汽车和房车里。
an offcut = 一块边角料

offence
/əˈfens/
核心：两支。一是违法行为、罪行，可数：commit an offence、a criminal offence、a first offence（初犯）；
二是冒犯、得罪，不可数：take offence 是见怪、生气，cause offence、give offence 是得罪人；
no offence 是（口语）没有冒犯的意思、别见怪。
体育里指进攻、进攻方（美式）。美式拼作 offense。
① He committed the offence while he was still on probation.
= 他犯下那桩罪行的时候，还在缓刑期内。
commit an offence = 犯罪
② Driving without insurance is a criminal offence in every country in Europe.
= 无保险驾驶在欧洲每个国家都是刑事犯罪。
a criminal offence = 刑事犯罪
③ Because it was a first offence, the court gave her a fine.
= 因为是初犯，法庭只判她罚款。
a first offence = 初犯
④ She took offence at the joke and left before the coffee.
= 她听了那个玩笑不高兴，咖啡没上就走了。
take offence = 见怪、生气
⑤ The poster was taken down because it had caused offence to many visitors.
= 那张海报得罪了许多来客，被摘了下来。
cause offence = 得罪人
⑥ No offence, but that hat does not suit you.
= 别见怪，那顶帽子不适合你。
no offence = 别见怪
⑦ The team has the best offense in the league and the worst defence.
= 那支球队有联盟里最好的进攻和最差的防守。
offense = 进攻（美式拼法）
offense = 违法行为、冒犯（offence 的美式拼法）

offend
/əˈfend/
核心：三支。一是冒犯、得罪：让人生气、难堪，多用被动；二是让人看着、听着不舒服；
三是（正式）犯罪、违法：不及物；offend against 是违背（原则、道德）。
形容词 offended 是生气的、觉得受了冒犯的；offending 作定语指惹出麻烦的那个；
名词 offender 是犯罪的人：a first offender、a young offender，也指惹麻烦的东西。并入本条。
① I hope that I did not offend your mother by leaving so early.
= 我走得那么早，但愿没有得罪你母亲。
offend somebody = 得罪某人
② She was deeply offended that nobody had asked her opinion.
= 没有人问她的意见，她大为不快。
be offended = 觉得受了冒犯
③ The bright orange shed offends the eye of everyone who walks past.
= 那座亮橙色的棚子，路过的人看着都觉得刺眼。
offend the eye = 看着刺眼
④ Many prisoners offend again within a year of being released.
= 许多囚犯获释不到一年就再次犯罪。
offend again = 再次犯罪
⑤ The proposal offends against the principle that everyone is equal before the law.
= 那项提议违背了法律面前人人平等的原则。
offend against something = 违背某事
⑥ He looked offended when I offered to pay for the meal.
= 我提出付饭钱的时候，他显得不高兴。
look offended = 显得不高兴
⑦ The dentist pulled out the offending tooth in under a minute.
= 牙医不到一分钟就把那颗惹祸的牙拔了。
the offending tooth = 惹祸的那颗牙
⑧ As a first offender, she was given a warning.
= 她是初犯，只受到警告。
a first offender = 初犯者
⑨ The scheme finds work for young offenders when they leave custody.
= 那项计划给出狱的少年犯找工作。
a young offender = 少年犯
an offender = 犯罪的人

offensive
/əˈfensɪv/
核心：形容词三支。一是冒犯人的、无礼的：offensive remarks；二是（正式）令人厌恶的：an offensive smell；
三是进攻的、攻击性的，只作定语：an offensive weapon，跟 defensive 相对。
名词是（军事上的）进攻、攻势，也指有组织的强势行动：launch an offensive；
go on the offensive 与 take the offensive 是先发制人、转守为攻；a charm offensive 是魅力攻势。
副词 offensively 与名词 offensiveness 并入本条。
① He was sent off for making offensive remarks to the referee.
= 他因为对裁判出言不逊被罚下场。
offensive remarks = 冒犯人的话
② Some viewers found the advertisement deeply offensive.
= 有些观众觉得那则广告非常冒犯人。
find something offensive = 觉得某事冒犯人
③ An offensive smell came from the bins behind the restaurant.
= 餐馆后面的垃圾桶散发出一股难闻的气味。
an offensive smell = 难闻的气味
④ He was charged with carrying an offensive weapon in a public place.
= 他被控在公共场所携带攻击性武器。
an offensive weapon = 攻击性武器
⑤ The army launched a major offensive along the northern front at dawn.
= 军队在黎明时沿北线发动了大规模进攻。
launch an offensive = 发动进攻
⑥ The minister went on the offensive and accused her critics of lying.
= 那位大臣转守为攻，指责批评她的人撒谎。
go on the offensive = 转守为攻
⑦ The company began a charm offensive to win over the local council.
= 那家公司发起了一场魅力攻势，想把当地市政会争取过来。
a charm offensive = 魅力攻势
⑧ The guest behaved offensively to the waiters all evening.
= 那位客人整个晚上对侍者都很无礼。
offensively = 无礼地
⑨ Readers complained about the offensiveness of the cartoon.
= 读者投诉那幅漫画冒犯人。
offensiveness = 冒犯

offer
/ˈɒfə(r)/
核心：动词四支。一是主动拿出、提供，由对方决定要不要：offer somebody something、offer something to somebody；
二是主动提出做某事：offer to do something；三是出价：offer somebody 加金额 for something；
四是提供、具备（机会、好处、服务）。
名词一是提议、主动表示：an offer of help、accept 或 turn down an offer；二是出价、报价：make an offer；
三是特价优惠：a special offer。
习语：on offer 一是可供选用，二是（英式）正在特价；under offer 是（英式，房子）已有人出价、待成交；
open to offers 是可以议价；have something to offer 是有可取之处。
名词 offering 指供品、献礼，也指推出的产品、作品，并入本条。
① She offered me a cup of tea and a seat by the fire.
= 她请我喝杯茶，让我坐到炉边。
offer somebody something = 请某人用某物、给某人某物
② The company offered the job to a candidate from outside.
= 公司把那个职位给了一位外来的应聘者。
offer something to somebody = 把某物提供给某人
③ A stranger offered to carry her bags up the steps.
= 一个陌生人主动提出帮她把包拎上台阶。
offer to do = 主动提出做某事
④ They offered us two hundred thousand for the house, and we refused.
= 他们出二十万买那座房子，我们没答应。
offer somebody a sum for something = 向某人出价买某物
⑤ The job offers good prospects but very little pay to begin with.
= 那份工作前景不错，只是起薪很低。
offer prospects = 有前景
⑥ Thank you for your kind offer of help, which I am glad to accept.
= 谢谢你好心提出帮忙，我乐于接受。
an offer of help = 帮忙的提议
⑦ He turned down an offer from a rival firm at twice the salary.
= 他回绝了一家对手公司开出的双倍薪水的聘请。
turn down an offer = 回绝提议
accept an offer = 接受提议
⑧ We made an offer on the flat and heard nothing for a week.
= 我们给那套公寓出了价，一个星期没有回音。
make an offer = 出价
⑨ The supermarket has a special offer on coffee until Sunday.
= 那家超市的咖啡特价到星期天。
offer = 优惠
a special offer = 特价优惠
⑩ There is a wide range of evening classes on offer at the college.
= 那所学院有各种各样的晚间课程可选。
on offer = 可供选用
⑪ Tinned tomatoes are on offer this week at three for a pound.
= 番茄罐头这个星期特价，一镑三听。
on offer = 正在特价
⑫ The cottage is already under offer, so we shall have to look elsewhere.
= 那座小屋已经有人出价了，我们只好另找。
under offer = 已有人出价、待成交
⑬ The owner wants ten thousand for the boat but is open to offers.
= 船主那条船要价一万，不过可以商量。
open to offers = 可以议价
⑭ The small town has a great deal to offer the visitor who likes walking.
= 对喜欢徒步的游客来说，那座小城大有可取之处。
have something to offer = 有可取之处
⑮ Visitors leave offerings of fruit and flowers at the foot of the statue.
= 来客在雕像脚下留下水果和鲜花作供品。
an offering = 供品
⑯ The band's latest offering is its weakest album for ten years.
= 那支乐队的最新作品，是他们十年来最弱的一张专辑。
somebody's latest offering = 某人的最新作品

offertory
/ˈɒfətri/
核心：两支，宗教用语，可数。一是（基督教礼拜中）奉献饼和酒的那一段仪式；二是礼拜时收的捐款，也指收捐款这个环节。
复数 offertories。
① The choir sang a short anthem during the offertory.
= 奉献礼进行的时候，唱诗班唱了一首短短的圣歌。
during the offertory = 奉献礼期间
② The offertory that Sunday went towards repairing the church roof.
= 那个星期天的奉献款用来修教堂的屋顶。
the offertory = 奉献款

offhand
/ˌɒfˈhænd/
核心：两支。一是形容词，漫不经心的、怠慢的：对人不够客气、不当回事，含贬义；二是副词，即刻地、不查资料凭记忆地，多用于否定与疑问。
副词 offhandedly 是漫不经心地，并入本条。
① The waiter was offhand with us and did not look up from his phone.
= 那个服务员对我们爱搭不理，眼睛都没离开手机。
be offhand with somebody = 对某人爱搭不理
② I cannot tell you offhand how many people came, but I can look it up.
= 来了多少人我一下子说不上来，不过可以查一查。
offhand = 一下子、不查资料
③ "It was nothing," she said offhandedly, and changed the subject.
= 「没什么。」她漫不经心地说，然后换了话题。
offhandedly = 漫不经心地

office
/ˈɒfɪs/
核心：五支。一是办公室、办公楼、办事处，可数：go to the office、the head office；也指在同一处办公的全体同事；
二是有专门用途的处所：a ticket office（售票处）、a post office（邮局）、a doctor's office（美式，诊所）；
三是政府的部、局，大写：the Home Office（英国内政部）；四是公职、官职，可数也可不可数：hold office；
take office 是就职，in office 是在任，leave office 是卸任，run for office 是竞选公职；
五是 through the good offices of，（正式）承蒙某人斡旋、帮忙。
以它打头的搭配并入本条：office block（办公大楼）、office hours（办公时间）、office worker（办公室职员）、office party（公司聚会）；
office boy、office girl 是旧时办公室里打杂的小伙计、小姑娘；office-holder 是任公职的人。
① She gets to the office at eight and leaves long after everyone else.
= 她八点到办公室，比谁都走得晚。
office = 办公室
get to the office = 到办公室
② The firm has its head office in Leeds and branches in six other cities.
= 那家公司总部设在利兹，另在六座城市有分支。
the head office = 总部
③ The whole office went out for lunch on her last day.
= 她上班的最后一天，整个办公室的人一起出去吃了午饭。
the whole office = 整个办公室的人
④ You can collect the tickets from the ticket office an hour before the concert.
= 音乐会开始前一小时，你可以到售票处取票。
a ticket office = 售票处
⑤ I must go to the post office before it closes at half past five.
= 我得赶在五点半邮局关门之前去一趟。
post office = 邮局
⑥ The Home Office refused to comment on the case.
= 内政部拒绝对此案置评。
the Home Office = 内政部
⑦ He has held public office for thirty years without a single scandal.
= 他任公职三十年，没出过一桩丑闻。
hold office = 任职
⑧ The new president takes office on the first day of the year.
= 新总统在元旦那天就职。
take office = 就职
⑨ The party has been in office for twelve years and looks tired.
= 那个党已经执政十二年，显出了疲态。
in office = 在任、执政
⑩ She has decided to run for office in the spring elections.
= 她决定在春季选举中竞选公职。
run for office = 竞选公职
⑪ The prisoners were freed through the good offices of a neutral ambassador.
= 承蒙一位中立国大使斡旋，那些囚犯获释了。
through the good offices of somebody = 承蒙某人斡旋
⑫ The old cinema was pulled down to make way for an office block.
= 那家老电影院被拆掉，给一栋办公大楼腾地方。
an office block = 办公大楼
⑬ Please telephone during office hours, between nine and five.
= 请在办公时间来电，九点到五点。
office hours = 办公时间
⑭ The trains at that hour are packed with office workers.
= 那个钟点的火车上挤满了上班族。
an office worker = 办公室职员
⑮ He made a fool of himself at the office party and stayed at home on Monday.
= 他在公司聚会上出了洋相，星期一没来上班。
the office party = 公司聚会
⑯ He joined the bank as an office boy at fourteen and retired as its manager.
= 他十四岁进银行当小杂役，退休时已是行长。
an office boy = 办公室小杂役
an office girl = 办公室小杂役（女）
⑰ The rule applies to every office-holder, from the mayor down.
= 那条规定适用于每一位任公职的人，从市长往下都算。
an office-holder = 任公职的人

officer
/ˈɒfɪsə(r)/
核心：三支，可数。一是军官：an army officer、a senior officer；二是警察、警官：a police officer，也用来当面称呼；
三是（政府、机构里）有职务的官员、干事、高级职员：a customs officer、a press officer。
跟 official 的分别：official 泛指官员，这个词多指穿制服的或担任具体职务的人。
① Her father was an army officer who moved the family every two years.
= 她父亲是陆军军官，每两年就带着全家搬一次。
an army officer = 陆军军官
officer = 警官、军官
② A police officer stopped the car and asked to see his licence.
= 一名警察拦下那辆车，要求看他的驾照。
a police officer = 警察
③ "Is there a problem, officer?" he asked, winding down the window.
= 「有什么问题吗，警官？」他摇下车窗问。
officer = 警官（当面称呼）
④ The customs officer opened every case and found nothing.
= 海关官员把每只箱子都打开了，什么也没查到。
a customs officer = 海关官员
officer = 有职务的人员
⑤ All questions from journalists should go to the press officer.
= 记者的问题一律找新闻干事。
a press officer = 新闻干事

official
/əˈfɪʃəl/
核心：形容词三支。一是官方的、正式的：由当权者发布或认可的，the official language；
二是公务的、职务上的：official duties、an official visit；三是对外宣称的（未必是真的）：the official reason。
名词是官员、高级职员，可数：a government official。
副词 officially 一是正式地、官方地，二是据官方说法、名义上。
名词 officialdom 是（贬）官僚、官场的总称；officialese 是官样文章、官腔。
英国的 official receiver 是法院指派的破产管理人；an official secret 是依法须保密的官方机密。并入本条。
① The country has two official languages, and every sign is printed in both.
= 该国有两种官方语言，每块牌子都用两种文字印。
official = 官方的
an official language = 官方语言
② The prime minister is on an official visit to Japan this week.
= 首相本周正式访问日本。
an official visit = 正式访问
③ The official reason for the delay was bad weather, but nobody believed it.
= 对外说延误是因为天气恶劣，可没人相信。
the official reason = 对外宣称的理由
④ A government official confirmed that the talks had broken down.
= 一名政府官员证实，谈判破裂了。
official = 官员（政府的）
a government official = 政府官员
⑤ The bridge was officially opened by the queen in the spring.
= 那座桥春天由女王正式剪彩通车。
officially = 正式地
⑥ Officially he is on holiday, but everyone knows that he has been sacked.
= 名义上他在休假，可人人都知道他被解雇了。
Officially, … = 名义上…
⑦ The plan was lost for a year in the corridors of officialdom.
= 那项计划在官僚机构的走廊里搁置了一年。
officialdom = 官僚、官场
⑧ The letter was written in such dense officialese that she read it three times.
= 那封信满纸官腔，她读了三遍。
officialese = 官样文章、官腔
⑨ The company's affairs are now in the hands of the official receiver.
= 那家公司的事务现由破产管理人接管。
the official receiver = 破产管理人
⑩ He was charged with passing an official secret to a newspaper.
= 他被控把官方机密透露给一家报纸。
an official secret = 官方机密

officiate
/əˈfɪʃieɪt/
核心：两支，正式。一是主持（宗教仪式、典礼）：介词用 at；二是（体育）执裁、当裁判。
① The bishop officiated at the wedding of the mayor's daughter.
= 主教主持了市长女儿的婚礼。
officiate at a wedding = 主持婚礼
② She was the first woman to officiate at a men's cup final.
= 她是头一位执裁男子杯赛决赛的女性。
officiate at a match = 执裁一场比赛

officious
/əˈfɪʃəs/
核心：爱指手画脚的、好摆官架子的：手里有点小权就过分认真、多管闲事。形容词，含贬义。
副词 officiously 与名词 officiousness 并入本条。
① An officious attendant told us off for standing on the wrong side of the rope.
= 一个爱管闲事的管理员因为我们站错了绳子那边，把我们训了一顿。
an officious attendant = 爱管闲事的管理员
② The clerk officiously demanded to see three separate proofs of address.
= 那个职员摆着官架子，非要看三种不同的住址证明。
officiously = 摆着官架子
③ The officiousness of the guards at the gate put many visitors off.
= 门口警卫的官架子让许多访客扫兴。
officiousness = 官架子

offing
/ˈɒfɪŋ/
核心：只见于习语 in the offing：即将发生的、快要来了的。本义是从岸上望得见的远处海面，船到了那儿就快进港了。
① With an election in the offing, no minister wants to raise taxes.
= 大选在即，哪个大臣都不想加税。
in the offing = 即将发生
② There are rumours of a pay rise in the offing for the junior staff.
= 有传言说，基层员工快要加薪了。
something in the offing = 快要来的某事

offline
/ˌɒfˈlaɪn/
核心：两支，形容词兼副词。一是没有连上互联网的、离线的，反义 online；二是（口语）不在网上的、现实生活里的；
take something offline 是把话题留到会后私下谈。
① You can download the map and use it offline in the mountains.
= 你可以把地图下载下来，到山里离线使用。
use something offline = 离线使用某物
② The whole system was offline for six hours after the power cut.
= 停电之后，整个系统掉线了六个小时。
be offline = 掉线、离线
③ The two bloggers had argued for years before they finally met offline.
= 那两位博主在网上吵了好多年，才终于在线下见了面。
meet offline = 线下见面
④ That is a detail, so let us take it offline and move on.
= 那是细节问题，咱们会后再谈，先往下进行。
take something offline = 留到会后私下谈

offload
/ˌɒfˈləʊd/
核心：两支。一是卸（货）、从车船上卸下；二是把不想要的东西、责任、烦恼甩给别人：介词用 on、onto。及物动词。
① The lorries offload at the back of the shop before the doors open.
= 卡车在开门之前到店后面卸货。
offload = 卸货
② The bank offloaded its bad loans onto a company set up for the purpose.
= 那家银行把坏账甩给了一家专门为此设立的公司。
offload something onto somebody = 把某物甩给某人
③ He rings his sister every evening to offload his worries about work.
= 他每天晚上给姐姐打电话，倾倒工作上的烦恼。
offload your worries = 倾倒烦恼

offprint
/ˈɒfprɪnt/
核心：抽印本：从期刊或文集里单独印出来的一篇文章，供作者分送同行。可数，学术出版用语。
① The author sent an offprint of the article to everyone she had quoted.
= 作者给她引用过的每个人都寄了一份那篇文章的抽印本。
an offprint of an article = 文章的抽印本
② His shelves are full of offprints signed by colleagues long dead.
= 他的书架上摆满了早已过世的同行签名送的抽印本。
offprints = 抽印本

offset
/ˈɒfset/
核心：动词：抵消、弥补：用一样东西平衡掉另一样的影响，介词用 against、by。过去式、过去分词都是 offset。
名词一是抵消的东西：a carbon offset 是碳抵消；二是胶印：一种印刷方法，也作定语。
① The rise in prices has been offset by a fall in the cost of fuel.
= 价格的上涨被燃料成本的下降抵消了。
offset = 抵消
be offset by something = 被某事抵消
② You can offset the cost of the computer against your tax.
= 你可以用买电脑的开支来抵税。
offset something against something = 用某物抵某物
③ The airline lets passengers buy a carbon offset with each ticket.
= 那家航空公司让乘客在买票时顺带买一份碳抵消。
a carbon offset = 碳抵消
④ The posters were printed by offset on heavy paper.
= 那些海报是用胶印印在厚纸上的。
offset printing = 胶印

offshoot
/ˈɒfʃuːt/
核心：两支，可数。一是分支、衍生出来的机构：从一个较大的组织、公司、运动里分出来的；二是（植物的）分枝、侧芽。
① The magazine began as an offshoot of a student newspaper.
= 那本杂志最初是一份学生报纸的分支。
an offshoot of something = 某事物的分支
② She cut an offshoot from the base of the plant and potted it up.
= 她从那株植物的根部剪下一个侧芽，栽进了花盆。
an offshoot = 侧芽

offshore
/ˌɒfˈʃɔː(r)/
核心：形容词兼副词，三支。一是在海上的、离岸的：offshore wind farms；二是（风）从陆地吹向海上的；
三是（金融）设在境外、尤指低税地区的：an offshore account。
动词是把业务、岗位转移到海外，名词 offshoring 并入本条。
① The country gets a tenth of its power from offshore wind farms.
= 该国十分之一的电来自海上风电场。
offshore wind farms = 海上风电场
② The ship anchored two miles offshore and sent a boat to the beach.
= 那艘船在离岸两英里处抛锚，派了一条小艇上海滩。
two miles offshore = 离岸两英里
③ An offshore breeze carried the smoke out to sea.
= 一阵离岸风把烟吹向了海上。
an offshore breeze = 离岸风
④ The money was hidden in an offshore account on a Caribbean island.
= 那笔钱藏在加勒比海一座岛上的离岸账户里。
an offshore account = 离岸账户
⑤ The bank has offshored its call centre and cut four hundred jobs at home.
= 那家银行把客服中心迁到了海外，在国内裁掉四百个岗位。
offshore something = 把某业务迁到海外
⑥ Offshoring has saved the firm money and lost it a good many customers.
= 业务外迁给那家公司省了钱，也让它丢了不少顾客。
offshoring = 业务外迁

offside
/ˌɒfˈsaɪd/
核心：两个用法。一是（足球、橄榄球、冰球）越位的：形容词、副词，名词指越位这件事或越位规则；
二是（英式）名词与形容词，车辆远离路边的一侧，在英国即右侧、驾驶座那一侧，反义 nearside。
① The goal was disallowed because the striker was offside.
= 那个进球被判无效，因为前锋越位了。
be offside = 越位
② She spent half the match explaining the offside rule to her father.
= 半场比赛，她都在给父亲讲越位规则。
the offside rule = 越位规则
③ The offside front tyre is almost bald and must be replaced.
= 右前轮胎几乎磨平了，必须换掉。
the offside tyre = 靠路中间一侧的轮胎
④ The van scraped the offside of the car as it squeezed past.
= 那辆货车挤过去的时候，刮了轿车靠路中间的那一侧。
the offside = 车辆远离路边的一侧

offspring
/ˈɒfsprɪŋ/
核心：后代、子女、（动物的）幼崽。单复数同形，不加 s；正式用语，说人时也带点诙谐。
① The female guards her offspring for the first six weeks of their lives.
= 雌性在幼崽出生后的头六个星期守护着它们。
her offspring = 它的幼崽
② All three of their offspring have left home and call on Sundays.
= 他们的三个孩子都离家了，星期天打电话来。
somebody's offspring = 某人的子女
③ A mule is the offspring of a male donkey and a female horse.
= 骡子是公驴和母马的后代。
the offspring of A and B = 甲和乙的后代

offstage
/ˌɒfˈsteɪdʒ/
核心：形容词兼副词，两支。一是在舞台之外的、观众看不见的地方的；二是（演员）不演戏的时候、私下里的。
① The murder happens offstage, and the audience hears only a scream.
= 那场谋杀发生在台外，观众只听见一声尖叫。
happen offstage = 发生在台外
② An offstage trumpet answers the soloist in the last movement.
= 末乐章里，台外的一支小号应和着独奏者。
an offstage trumpet = 台外的小号
③ The comedian is shy and rather serious offstage.
= 那位喜剧演员下了台很腼腆，还相当严肃。
offstage = 下了台、私下里

often
/ˈɒfən/（常见）/ˈɒftən/（也读）
核心：副词，经常、常常；也指「在很多情形下」。位置在实义动词前、助动词和 be 后面；也可放句末，尤其前面有 very、quite、so 时。
比较级 more often，最高级 most often；oftener 也有人用。How often…? 问频率。
习语：every so often 是时不时；more often than not 与 as often as not 是多半、十有六七；all too often 是太常见了（含惋惜）；
once too often 是（做冒险的事）终于栽了一回。
旧式、美式口语的 oftentimes 意思相同，并入本条。
① We often walk along the river on Sunday mornings.
= 我们星期天早上常沿着河边散步。
often = 经常
② She is often late, but she always brings cake.
= 她常迟到，不过总带着蛋糕来。
be often late = 常迟到
③ How often do the buses run after six o'clock?
= 六点以后，公共汽车多久一班？
How often = 多久一次
④ I do not see my cousins very often since they moved north.
= 自从表亲们搬到北方，我就不大见得着他们了。
not very often = 不大经常
⑤ Old houses are often colder than new ones.
= 老房子往往比新房子冷。
often = 往往
这里指「在很多情形下」，不指次数。
⑥ Every so often a train passes and the whole house shakes.
= 时不时有火车经过，整座房子都跟着抖。
every so often = 时不时
⑦ More often than not, the simplest explanation turns out to be right.
= 最简单的解释，多半到头来是对的。
more often than not = 多半
⑧ As often as not, he forgets his keys and has to ring the bell.
= 他十有六七忘带钥匙，只好按门铃。
as often as not = 十有六七
⑨ All too often, the people who need help most are the last to ask for it.
= 最需要帮助的人，往往最后一个开口，这种事太常见了。
all too often = 太常见了
⑩ He drove home drunk once too often and lost his licence.
= 他酒后开车回家，终于栽了一回，被吊销了驾照。
once too often = 终于栽了一回
⑪ Oftentimes the old man would sit on the porch until the stars came out.
= 那位老人常常在门廊上坐到星星出来。
oftentimes = 常常（旧式）

ogle
/ˈəʊɡəl/
核心：色眯眯地盯着看：带着明显的性兴趣打量别人，让对方不舒服。及物动词，也接 at，含贬义。
① She was tired of being ogled by the men at the bar.
= 她受够了被吧台边的男人色眯眯地盯着看。
be ogled by somebody = 被某人色眯眯地盯着
② He sat on the wall ogling at the girls as they left the office.
= 他坐在矮墙上，色眯眯地盯着下班出来的姑娘们。
ogle at somebody = 色眯眯地盯着某人

ogre
/ˈəʊɡə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是（童话里的）食人巨妖、吃人怪；二是（比喻）凶恶可怕的人。
女性叫 ogress，并入本条。
① In the story an ogre lives under the bridge and eats travellers.
= 故事里，桥底下住着一个吃过路人的巨妖。
an ogre = 食人巨妖
② The new headmaster is not the ogre that the children had expected.
= 新校长并不像孩子们原先以为的那样凶神恶煞。
be an ogre = 凶神恶煞
③ The ogress in the fairy tale keeps her gold in a kitchen pot.
= 童话里的那个女妖把金子藏在厨房的一口锅里。
an ogress = 女妖

oh
/əʊ/
核心：感叹词，表示吃惊、失望、高兴、恍然大悟等一时的情绪，也用来在回话前稍作停顿、引起注意。书面里后面加逗号或感叹号。
Oh dear! 是哎呀、糟糕；Oh well 是算了、那也没办法；Oh no! 是糟了；Oh yes? 是是吗（表示怀疑）。
口语的 oh-oh（也写作 uh-oh）表示出了点岔子；oh-so 放在形容词前，是带讽刺的「那么地」；旧式的 oho 表示突然明白或得意。并入本条。
① Oh, what a lovely surprise! I thought you were still in Scotland.
= 啊，真是惊喜！我还以为你在苏格兰呢。
Oh, … = 啊，…
② Oh dear, I seem to have left my purse at home.
= 哎呀，我好像把钱包落在家里了。
Oh dear! = 哎呀、糟糕
③ "The shop is shut." "Oh well, we can come back tomorrow."
= 「店关门了。」「算了，咱们明天再来吧。」
Oh well = 算了、那也没办法
④ "I shall finish it by Friday." "Oh yes? You said that last week."
= 「我星期五之前做完。」「是吗？你上星期也这么说。」
Oh yes? = 是吗？
⑤ "Where do you live?" "Oh, not far from here, near the park."
= 「你住哪儿？」「哦，离这儿不远，公园附近。」
Oh, … = 哦，…
回话前稍作停顿。
⑥ Oh-oh, the baby has found the box of paints.
= 糟了，宝宝找到那盒颜料了。
oh-oh = 糟了
⑦ He gave one of his oh-so charming smiles and asked for a discount.
= 他露出他那迷人得不得了的笑容，开口要折扣。
oh-so = （带讽刺）那么地
⑧ "Oho, so that is where the biscuits went," said her grandfather.
= 她爷爷说：「哦嗬，原来饼干到这儿来了。」
oho = 哦嗬（恍然大悟）

ohm
/əʊm/
核心：欧姆：电阻的单位，符号 Ω。可数，以德国物理学家的姓氏命名。测电阻的仪表叫 ohmmeter，并入本条。
① The heating element has a resistance of about twenty ohms.
= 那个发热元件的电阻大约是二十欧姆。
twenty ohms = 二十欧姆
② He checked the broken wire with an ohmmeter and found where it had snapped.
= 他用欧姆表查那根断线，找到了断的地方。
an ohmmeter = 欧姆表

oi
/ɔɪ/
核心：感叹词，英式口语：喂！用来大声引起别人注意，口气不客气，常带恼火。只用于喊话，对陌生人、长辈用是失礼的。
① "Oi! You cannot park there, mate," shouted the man from the garage.
= 修车铺里的人喊道：「喂！伙计，那儿不能停车。」
Oi! = 喂！
② "Oi, that is my seat," said the boy, pointing at his coat on the back of it.
= 「喂，那是我的座位。」男孩指着搭在椅背上的外套说。
Oi, … = 喂，…

oik
/ɔɪk/
核心：粗人、没教养的家伙：自以为高人一等的人对下层人的蔑称。可数，英式口语，含阶层偏见，用的人自己显得势利。
① The old colonel called the builders next door a bunch of oiks.
= 那个老上校把隔壁干活的建筑工人叫作一帮粗人。
a bunch of oiks = 一帮粗人
② In the comedy a rich family is horrified when the daughter marries an oik.
= 那出喜剧里，一户有钱人家的女儿嫁了个粗人，全家大惊失色。
an oik = 粗人

oil
/ɔɪl/
核心：名词四支。一是石油、原油，不可数：drill for oil、strike oil（钻到石油，引申为一下子发了财）；
二是燃油、润滑油，不可数：check the oil 是查机油；三是食用油、护肤用的油，可数也可不可数：olive oil；
四是 oils，复数，油画颜料：paint in oils；an oil 是一幅油画。
动词是给…上油。形容词 oiled 是上过油的：well oiled 既指运转顺畅，英式口语里也指喝醉了。
习语：burn the midnight oil 是开夜车；pour oil on troubled waters 是平息风波、劝架；oil the wheels 是（英式）从中疏通、让事情顺利；
be no oil painting 是（英式，诙谐）长得不好看。
以它打头的复合词并入本条：oil paint（油画颜料）、oil painting（油画）、oil rig（石油钻井平台）、oil well（油井）、
oil tanker（油轮）、oil slick（海面上的浮油）、oil pan（美式，发动机的油底壳）、oil-fired（烧油的）、oil-bearing（含油的）；
oilseed rape 是（英式）油菜。
① The country earns most of its income from oil and gas.
= 该国的大部分收入来自石油和天然气。
oil and gas = 石油和天然气
这一支不可数，指地下开采出来的原油。
② The price of oil doubled in the space of six months.
= 油价在六个月里翻了一番。
the price of oil = 油价
③ The company struck oil in the desert after five dry wells.
= 打了五口干井之后，那家公司在沙漠里钻到了石油。
strike oil = 钻到石油、一下子发了财
④ Check the oil and the tyres before a long journey.
= 出远门之前，查一查机油和轮胎。
check the oil = 查机油
这里指发动机里的润滑油。
⑤ Fry the onions gently in a little olive oil until they are soft.
= 用少许橄榄油把洋葱小火煎软。
olive oil = 橄榄油
说种类时可数：cooking oils 是各种食用油。
⑥ She rubbed a few drops of oil into the dry skin on her hands.
= 她往手上干燥的皮肤上揉了几滴油。
rub oil into something = 往某处揉油
⑦ He paints in oils and has never tried watercolour.
= 他画油画，从没试过水彩。
paint in oils = 画油画
复数形式，指油画颜料。
⑧ The hinges need oiling, because the gate squeals every time it opens.
= 合页该上油了，那扇门一开就吱吱叫。
oil something = 给某物上油
这里是动词。
⑨ The office runs like a well-oiled machine when she is in charge.
= 她当家的时候，办公室运转得像一台上足了油的机器。
a well-oiled machine = 运转顺畅的机器
英式口语里说人 well oiled，是喝得不少了。
⑩ He burned the midnight oil for a month before the exams.
= 考试前，他开了一个月的夜车。
burn the midnight oil = 开夜车
本义是点着油灯干到半夜。
⑪ Her mother poured oil on troubled waters by changing the subject.
= 她母亲换了个话题，把风波平息了。
pour oil on troubled waters = 平息风波
⑫ A few friendly phone calls oiled the wheels before the formal talks.
= 正式会谈之前，几个友好的电话起了疏通的作用。
oil the wheels = 从中疏通
⑬ He is no oil painting, but he has a kind face.
= 他长得不好看，不过面相和善。
no oil painting = 长得不好看
英式的玩笑说法，口气不算重。
⑭ The tubes of oil paint had dried hard in the attic.
= 那几管油画颜料在阁楼里干硬了。
oil paint = 油画颜料
oil colour = 油画颜料
⑮ An oil painting of her grandfather hangs above the fireplace.
= 壁炉上方挂着一幅她祖父的油画像。
an oil painting = 油画
⑯ He works three weeks on an oil rig and then has three weeks at home.
= 他在石油钻井平台上干三个星期，然后回家歇三个星期。
an oil rig = 石油钻井平台
⑰ The first oil well in the region was drilled in 1908.
= 该地区的头一口油井是一九〇八年钻的。
an oil well = 油井
⑱ An oil tanker ran aground on the rocks during the storm.
= 一艘油轮在风暴中触礁搁浅。
an oil tanker = 油轮
⑲ The oil slick spread for twenty miles and reached the beaches within a week.
= 浮油扩散了二十英里，一个星期内漂到了海滩。
an oil slick = 浮油
多指油轮、钻井平台泄漏造成的。
⑳ The mechanic found a crack in the oil pan and a puddle under the car.
= 修车工发现油底壳上有道裂缝，车底下一摊油。
an oil pan = 油底壳
㉑ The school still has an oil-fired boiler from the nineteen-sixties.
= 那所学校还在用一台二十世纪六十年代的燃油锅炉。
oil-fired = 烧油的
㉒ The survey found oil-bearing rock two kilometres below the sea bed.
= 勘测在海床以下两公里处发现了含油岩层。
oil-bearing = 含油的
㉓ In May the fields of oilseed rape turn the whole valley yellow.
= 五月，一片片油菜田把整条山谷染成黄色。
oilseed rape = 油菜
英式说法，北美叫 canola。

oilcan
/ˈɔɪlkæn/
核心：油壶：装润滑油的金属小罐，带一根细长的嘴，用来给机器的缝隙点油。可数。
① He kept an oilcan on the shelf above the lathe.
= 他在车床上方的架子上放着一只油壶。
an oilcan = 油壶
② A drop from the oilcan stopped the bicycle chain squeaking.
= 油壶里滴出一滴油，自行车链条就不吱吱响了。
a drop from the oilcan = 油壶里的一滴油

oilcloth
/ˈɔɪlklɒθ/
核心：油布：一面涂了油或树脂、防水的棉布，从前用来铺桌面、铺架子。不可数。
① The kitchen table was covered with a piece of checked oilcloth.
= 厨房的桌子上铺着一块格子油布。
a piece of oilcloth = 一块油布
② She wiped the oilcloth with a damp rag after every meal.
= 每顿饭后，她都用湿抹布把油布擦一遍。
wipe the oilcloth = 擦油布

oilfield
/ˈɔɪlfiːld/
核心：油田：地下或海底蕴藏石油、已在开采的一片区域。可数。
① The oilfield under the sea was discovered in the nineteen-seventies.
= 海底的那片油田是二十世纪七十年代发现的。
an oilfield = 油田
② Thousands of workers moved north when the new oilfields opened.
= 新油田一开，成千上万的工人往北迁。
the new oilfields = 新油田

oilman
/ˈɔɪlmæn/
核心：石油商、石油业的人：拥有石油公司的人，也指在油田干活的工人。可数，复数 oilmen。
① The ranch was bought by a Texas oilman who had never ridden a horse.
= 那座牧场被一个从没骑过马的得克萨斯石油商买下了。
a Texas oilman = 得克萨斯石油商
② The oilmen on the rig work twelve-hour shifts in all weathers.
= 钻井平台上的石油工人不论天气好坏，一班干十二个小时。
the oilmen on the rig = 钻井平台上的石油工人

oilskin
/ˈɔɪlskɪn/
核心：两支。一是油布、防水油布料：用油处理过的防水棉布，不可数；二是油布雨衣，可数；复数 oilskins 指整套油布雨衣裤，水手、渔民穿的。
① The lifeboat crew pulled on their oilskins as they ran down the slipway.
= 救生艇的船员一边顺着船台往下跑，一边套上油布雨衣裤。
oilskins = 油布雨衣裤
② An old yellow oilskin hung on a nail by the back door.
= 后门边的一颗钉子上挂着一件旧的黄色油布雨衣。
an oilskin = 油布雨衣

oily
/ˈɔɪli/
核心：三支。一是含油的、沾满油的、像油的：an oily rag、oily fish（鲑鱼、鲭鱼这类油性鱼）、oily skin；
二是（贬）油滑的、巴结得过头的：说人的举止、笑容。比较级 oilier。名词 oiliness 并入本条。
① He wiped his hands on an oily rag and shut the bonnet.
= 他在一块油乎乎的破布上擦了擦手，合上了发动机盖。
an oily rag = 油乎乎的破布
② Doctors recommend eating oily fish such as mackerel twice a week.
= 医生建议每周吃两次鲭鱼这类油性鱼。
oily fish = 油性鱼
③ The soap is made for people with oily skin.
= 那种香皂是给油性皮肤的人做的。
oily skin = 油性皮肤
④ The salesman had an oily smile that she did not trust.
= 那个推销员一脸油滑的笑，她信不过。
an oily smile = 油滑的笑
⑤ A squeeze of lemon cuts the oiliness of the sauce.
= 挤一点柠檬汁，能解掉酱汁的油腻。
oiliness = 油腻

oink
/ɔɪŋk/
核心：两支。一是名词、感叹词，哼哼：模仿猪叫的声音，多见于儿童读物；二是动词，（猪）哼哼叫。
① The toy pig says "oink" when you press its stomach.
= 那只玩具猪，按一下肚子就会哼哼叫。
say oink = 哼哼叫
② The piglets oinked and pushed one another at the trough.
= 小猪们在食槽边哼哼叫着，你推我挤。
piglets oink = 小猪哼哼叫

ointment
/ˈɔɪntmənt/
核心：药膏、软膏：涂在皮肤上治伤、止痒的油性药。可数也可不可数。
习语 a fly in the ointment 是美中不足、煞风景的一点小事。
① The nurse rubbed some ointment into the burn and covered it with gauze.
= 护士往烫伤处抹了些药膏，再盖上纱布。
rub ointment into something = 往某处抹药膏
② Apply the ointment twice a day until the rash has gone.
= 每天涂两次药膏，直到皮疹消退。
apply the ointment = 涂药膏
③ The only fly in the ointment was the noise from the road at night.
= 唯一美中不足的，是夜里路上的噪音。
a fly in the ointment = 美中不足之处

Ojibwa
/əʊˈdʒɪbwɑː/
核心：两支，首字母大写。一是奥吉布瓦人：北美五大湖一带的一支原住民，可数，复数不变或加 s；二是奥吉布瓦语，不可数。也拼作 Ojibwe。
① The Ojibwa have harvested wild rice from these lakes for many centuries.
= 许多个世纪以来，奥吉布瓦人一直在这些湖里采收野生稻。
the Ojibwa = 奥吉布瓦人
② The school teaches Ojibwa to children whose grandparents were punished for speaking it.
= 那所学校教孩子们奥吉布瓦语，而他们的祖辈曾因说这种语言受罚。
teach Ojibwa = 教奥吉布瓦语

OK
/ˌəʊˈkeɪ/
核心：口语里用得极多，也拼作 okay。五种用法。
一是感叹词：好的、行（表示同意）；也用来开头、转换话题，或加在句末问「行吗？」。
二是形容词：没事的、平安的；可以的、允许的：it is OK to do something；还过得去的、一般的；OK with、OK by 是某人没意见。
三是副词：还行、不错地：do OK。四是名词：批准、同意：give the OK、get the OK。
五是动词：批准：过去式 OK'd 或 okayed。
英式口语里更随意的说法是 okey-doke、okey-dokey。
① "Shall we meet at seven?" "OK, I will book a table."
= 「我们七点见好吗？」「好的，我来订位子。」
OK = 好的、行
② OK, let us move on to the second item on the list.
= 好，咱们接着看清单上的第二项。
OK, … = 好，…
用来开头、转换话题。
③ I shall be back by ten, OK?
= 我十点之前回来，行吗？
…, OK? = …，行吗？
④ Are you OK? You look very pale.
= 你没事吧？你脸色很苍白。
be OK = 没事、平安
这里是形容词，只作表语。
⑤ Is it OK to park here for an hour?
= 在这儿停一个钟头行吗？
it is OK to do something = 可以做某事
⑥ The hotel was OK, but I would not stay there again.
= 那家旅馆还凑合，不过我不会再去住了。
be OK = 还凑合
⑦ If it is OK with you, I shall leave a little early today.
= 你要是没意见，我今天早走一会儿。
OK with somebody = 某人没意见
⑧ She did OK in the exam, though not as well as she had hoped.
= 她考得还行，虽然没有预想的那么好。
do OK = 做得还行
这里是副词。
⑨ We cannot start building until the council gives us the OK.
= 市政会不点头，我们没法开工。
give somebody the OK = 给某人放行
get the OK = 获得批准
⑩ The manager has OK'd the plan, so we can order the parts.
= 经理批准了那个方案，我们可以订零件了。
OK something = 批准某事
这里是动词，过去式写作 OK'd。
⑪ "Okay," she wrote back, "but only if you bring the wine."
= 她回信说：「行，不过得你带酒。」
okay = 好的（OK 的另一种拼法）
⑫ "Can you watch the shop for five minutes?" "Okey-doke."
= 「你能帮我看五分钟店吗？」「好嘞。」
okey-doke = 好嘞

okapi
/əʊˈkɑːpi/
核心：㺢㹢狓：刚果雨林里的一种动物，是长颈鹿的近亲，身体深褐色，腿上有斑马似的条纹。可数，复数 okapis 或不变。
① The okapi was unknown to science until the first years of the twentieth century.
= 直到二十世纪头几年，科学界才知道有㺢㹢狓这种动物。
the okapi = 㺢㹢狓
② An okapi uses its long tongue to strip the leaves from a branch.
= 㺢㹢狓用它的长舌头把树枝上的叶子捋下来。
an okapi = 一头㺢㹢狓

okra
/ˈəʊkrə/
核心：秋葵：一种蔬菜，绿色的细长嫩荚，切开后有黏液，西非、美国南方、中东菜里常用。不可数，别名 lady's fingers、gumbo。
① She added sliced okra to the stew to thicken it.
= 她往炖菜里加了切片的秋葵，让汤汁变稠。
sliced okra = 切片的秋葵
② Fry the okra quickly over a high heat so that it stays crisp.
= 用大火把秋葵快炒一下，这样能保持脆嫩。
fry the okra = 炒秋葵

old
/əʊld/
核心：形容词，比较级 older，最高级 oldest；说家庭成员的长幼时另有 elder、eldest，只作定语。
一是年老的、上了年纪的，反义 young；the old 指老年人；当面说人 old 不客气，委婉说 elderly。
二是…岁的、有…年历史的：数字加 years old；How old…? 问年龄。
三是旧的、用了很久的、古老的，反义 new。四是从前的、原先的，只作定语：my old school。
五是相识已久的：an old friend，不指年纪。
六是口语里加在名词前表示亲昵、同情或随便：good old、poor old；any old 是随便哪个。
习语：the good old days 是过去的好时光；old hat 是老一套；as old as the hills 是古老得很；of old 是（文学）从前、早就。
以它打头的搭配很多，并入本条：old age（老年）、old-age pension 与 old-age pensioner（英式，养老金与领养老金的人）、
old people's home（养老院）；old-fashioned（老式的、过时的）、old-style、old-time（旧式的）、old-world（古色古香的）、
old-established（老字号的）；old school 指守旧的一派，old school tie 是（英式）名校校友的领带，引申为校友之间互相关照的关系网；
old hand（老手）、old-timer（老资格、老人）、old stager（英式，老手）、old master（十三至十七世纪欧洲的绘画大师，也指他们的画）；
old man 是（口语）老爸、老公，old woman 是老妈、老婆，也指婆婆妈妈的男人；old maid 是（旧，冒犯）老姑娘；
old girl 与 old boy 是（英式）校友；英式口语里带不敬的 old bat（讨厌的老太婆）、old buffer（老糊涂）、old dear（老太太）、
old lag（惯犯）也属这一类。仿古的拼法 olde 见于店名，olde worlde 是刻意做旧、古色古香得过头的。
① An old man was feeding the pigeons in the square.
= 一位老人在广场上喂鸽子。
an old man = 老人
② My grandmother is too old to manage the stairs.
= 我奶奶年纪太大，上不了楼梯了。
too old to do something = 年纪太大做不了某事
③ The benefit is meant to help the old and the sick.
= 那项补助是为了帮助老人和病人。
the old = 老年人
④ Their daughter is six years old and has just started school.
= 他们的女儿六岁，刚上学。
… years old = …岁
⑤ How old is the church at the top of the hill?
= 山顶上的那座教堂有多少年了？
How old…? = 多大、多少年了
⑥ She is three years older than her brother.
= 她比弟弟大三岁。
older than somebody = 比某人年长
说年龄的比较用 older；elder 只放在名词前。
⑦ He wore an old coat with a hole in the elbow.
= 他穿着一件肘部有洞的旧外套。
an old coat = 旧外套
⑧ The old part of the town lies inside the walls.
= 老城区在城墙里面。
the old part of the town = 老城区
⑨ I met my old headmaster on the train yesterday.
= 我昨天在火车上碰见了我从前的校长。
my old headmaster = 我从前的校长
这一支指「从前的」，不指年纪。
⑩ We are old friends and have known each other since school.
= 我们是老朋友，上学时就认识了。
an old friend = 老朋友
⑪ Good old Harry had kept two seats for us at the front.
= 还是哈里够意思，在前排给我们留了两个座位。
good old somebody = 还是某人够意思
⑫ Poor old Tom has broken his leg again.
= 可怜的汤姆又把腿摔断了。
poor old somebody = 可怜的某人
⑬ Do not use just any old paint on the window frames.
= 窗框可别随便拿什么漆来刷。
any old = 随便哪个
⑭ People talk about the good old days and forget the outdoor toilets.
= 人们念叨过去的好时光，却忘了那时的厕所在户外。
the good old days = 过去的好时光
⑮ The idea seemed daring ten years ago and is old hat today.
= 那个主意十年前显得大胆，如今已是老一套。
old hat = 老一套
⑯ The joke is as old as the hills, but the children still laugh.
= 那个笑话古老得很，孩子们照样笑。
as old as the hills = 古老得很
⑰ In days of old, the bridge was guarded by a toll keeper.
= 从前，那座桥有个收过桥费的人把守。
of old = 从前
⑱ He took up painting in his old age and sold his first picture at eighty.
= 他到老年才学画，八十岁卖出了头一幅。
old age = 老年
⑲ She lives on her old-age pension and a little rent from the flat upstairs.
= 她靠养老金和楼上那套公寓的一点租金生活。
an old-age pension = 养老金
an old-age pensioner = 领养老金的人
⑳ His mother moved into an old people's home near the sea.
= 他母亲搬进了海边的一家养老院。
an old people's home = 养老院
㉑ He has old-fashioned ideas about who should do the cooking.
= 在该谁做饭这件事上，他的想法很老派。
old-fashioned = 老式的、过时的
㉒ The band plays old-time dance music in an old-style ballroom.
= 那支乐队在一间旧式舞厅里演奏老式舞曲。
old-time = 旧式的
old-style = 旧式的
㉓ The village has an old-world charm that draws painters every summer.
= 那座村子有一种古色古香的韵味，每年夏天都引来画家。
old-world = 古色古香的
㉔ The work went to an old-established firm of builders in the next town.
= 那项工程交给了邻镇一家老字号的建筑公司。
old-established = 老字号的
㉕ The colonel is one of the old school and still writes letters by hand.
= 那位上校是守旧的一派，至今还用手写信。
the old school = 守旧的一派
㉖ He got the job through the old school tie rather than on merit.
= 他得到那份工作靠的是校友关系，不是凭本事。
the old school tie = 校友之间的关系网
㉗ Ask Maria, because she is an old hand at dealing with the tax office.
= 问玛丽亚吧，跟税务局打交道她是老手。
an old hand = 老手
㉘ The old-timers in the village remember when the road was a cart track.
= 村里的老人还记得那条路从前是条车辙土路。
an old-timer = 老资格、老人
㉙ The captain is an old stager who has played in six finals.
= 队长是个老将，打过六次决赛。
an old stager = 老手
㉚ The gallery owns two old masters and a room of modern prints.
= 那家画廊藏有两幅古典大师的画和一屋子现代版画。
an old master = 古典大师、大师的画
㉛ My old man taught me to fish on this stretch of the river.
= 我老爸就是在这一段河上教我钓鱼的。
somebody's old man = 某人的老爸、老公
㉜ Stop fussing like an old woman and get in the car.
= 别像个婆婆妈妈的人似的啰嗦了，上车吧。
an old woman = 婆婆妈妈的人
㉝ The novel mocks the way an unmarried aunt was dismissed as an old maid.
= 那部小说讽刺了从前把未婚的姑妈一概贬作老姑娘的做法。
an old maid = 老姑娘（旧称，冒犯）
㉞ The school invites its old girls and old boys back every June.
= 那所学校每年六月请校友回校。
an old girl = 女校友
an old boy = 男校友
㉟ The children thought their neighbour an old bat until she mended their bicycle.
= 孩子们原先觉得那位邻居是个讨厌的老太婆，直到她帮他们修好了自行车。
an old bat = 讨厌的老太婆
㊱ Some old buffer at the club insists on the same chair every day.
= 俱乐部里有个老糊涂，天天非坐同一把椅子不可。
an old buffer = 老糊涂
㊲ The old dear at the post office remembers everyone's birthday.
= 邮局里的那位老太太记得每个人的生日。
an old dear = 老太太
㊳ An old lag in the next cell showed him how the prison worked.
= 隔壁牢房的一个老犯人给他讲了监狱里的门道。
an old lag = 惯犯、老犯人
㊴ The tea room is called Ye Olde Kettle and is full of plastic beams.
= 那家茶室起了个仿古的店名，叫「老水壶」，里面全是塑料做的假梁。
olde = 仿古的 old（店名用）
㊵ The pub is a little too olde worlde, with horse brasses on every wall.
= 那家酒馆古色古香得有点过头，每面墙上都挂着马具铜饰。
olde worlde = 刻意做旧的

olden
/ˈəʊldən/
核心：往昔的、古时候的。形容词，只作定语，文学或诙谐，几乎只见于 in olden days、in olden times。
① In olden days the village baker lit his oven at two in the morning.
= 从前，村里的面包师凌晨两点就生炉子。
in olden days = 从前
② The children love stories of knights and castles in olden times.
= 孩子们爱听古时候骑士和城堡的故事。
in olden times = 古时候

oldie
/ˈəʊldi/
核心：两支，口语，可数。一是老歌、老片子：a golden oldie 是经典老歌；二是上了年纪的人，带点玩笑。
① The radio station plays nothing but oldies from the sixties and seventies.
= 那家电台只放六七十年代的老歌。
oldies = 老歌
② The band finished with a golden oldie that had everyone on their feet.
= 乐队用一首经典老歌收尾，全场都站了起来。
a golden oldie = 经典老歌
③ The dance class is full of oldies who put the young ones to shame.
= 舞蹈班里全是让年轻人自愧不如的老人家。
oldies = 老人家

oldish
/ˈəʊldɪʃ/
核心：有点老的、稍旧的。形容词，口语，说人说物都行。
① An oldish man in a raincoat was waiting by the gate.
= 一个穿雨衣、年纪稍大的男人在大门口等着。
an oldish man = 年纪稍大的男人
② They drive an oldish car that has never once let them down.
= 他们开一辆有点旧的车，那车从没掉过链子。
an oldish car = 有点旧的车

oldster
/ˈəʊldstə(r)/
核心：老人、上了年纪的人。可数，口语，带诙谐，主要是美式，仿 youngster 造的词。
① The beach was full of oldsters playing chess under umbrellas.
= 海滩上满是在遮阳伞底下下棋的老人家。
oldsters = 老人家
② A sprightly oldster of eighty won the dancing prize.
= 一位八十岁的硬朗老人赢得了舞蹈奖。
a sprightly oldster = 硬朗的老人

oleaginous
/ˌəʊliˈædʒɪnəs/
核心：两支，正式。一是含油的、油质的、油腻的；二是（贬）油滑的、满嘴奉承的。
① The seeds are oleaginous and are pressed for cooking oil.
= 那些种子含油，可以榨食用油。
be oleaginous = 含油的
② An oleaginous salesman followed us round the showroom praising our taste.
= 一个油滑的推销员跟着我们在展厅里转，一路夸我们有眼光。
an oleaginous salesman = 油滑的推销员

oleander
/ˌəʊliˈændə(r)/
核心：夹竹桃：地中海一带的常绿灌木，开红、粉、白色的花，全株有毒。可数也可不可数。
① Pink oleanders line the road from the airport to the town.
= 从机场到镇上的路两旁种着粉红的夹竹桃。
oleanders = 夹竹桃
② The gardener warned the children never to chew a leaf of oleander.
= 园丁告诫孩子们千万别嚼夹竹桃的叶子。
a leaf of oleander = 夹竹桃叶

olfactory
/ɒlˈfæktəri/
核心：嗅觉的、与闻气味有关的。形容词，只作定语，正式或科学用语。
① The olfactory nerve carries signals from the nose to the brain.
= 嗅神经把信号从鼻子传到大脑。
the olfactory nerve = 嗅神经
② A dog lives in an olfactory world that we can hardly imagine.
= 狗生活在一个我们几乎无法想象的气味世界里。
an olfactory world = 气味的世界

oligarch
/ˈɒlɪɡɑːk/
核心：寡头：寡头统治集团的一员；如今多指靠资源、垄断暴富并有很大政治影响的商人，尤其用于俄罗斯。可数。
名词 oligarchy 指寡头政治、由少数人掌权的国家，也指掌权的那一小撮人，并入本条。
① The football club was bought by a Russian oligarch with interests in oil.
= 那家足球俱乐部被一个有石油生意的俄罗斯寡头买下了。
a Russian oligarch = 俄罗斯寡头
② The city was ruled for centuries by an oligarchy of merchant families.
= 那座城几百年间由几个商人家族组成的寡头集团统治。
an oligarchy = 寡头集团、寡头政治

oligopoly
/ˌɒlɪˈɡɒpəli/
核心：寡头垄断：一个行业由少数几家大公司控制的局面，它们彼此竞争有限。可数，经济学用语，复数 oligopolies。
① The supermarket trade is an oligopoly of four chains.
= 超市这一行是四家连锁店的寡头垄断。
an oligopoly = 寡头垄断
② Prices stay high because the oligopoly has no reason to cut them.
= 价格居高不下，因为那几家垄断寡头没有降价的理由。
the oligopoly = 那几家垄断寡头

olive
/ˈɒlɪv/
核心：三支。一是橄榄、油橄榄：一种小小的椭圆形果实，绿的或黑的，可吃可榨油，可数；olive oil 是橄榄油；
二是橄榄树，也说 olive tree；三是橄榄绿，也作形容词；说肤色时指浅褐带黄的。
习语 olive branch 是橄榄枝、和解的表示：hold out an olive branch。
olive drab 是军装用的草绿色，也指这种颜色的军服。
① She put a bowl of green olives on the table with the drinks.
= 她端上饮料，配了一碗青橄榄。
green olives = 青橄榄
② Dress the salad with olive oil and a little lemon juice.
= 用橄榄油和一点柠檬汁拌沙拉。
olive oil = 橄榄油
③ The hillside is planted with olive trees that are hundreds of years old.
= 山坡上种着树龄几百年的橄榄树。
an olive tree = 橄榄树
④ He wore an olive jacket that matched the colour of his eyes.
= 他穿着一件橄榄绿的上衣，跟他眼睛的颜色很配。
an olive jacket = 橄榄绿的上衣
⑤ She has the olive skin and dark hair of her Italian mother.
= 她像她的意大利母亲，浅褐色皮肤，深色头发。
olive skin = 浅褐色的皮肤
⑥ The union held out an olive branch by offering to return to the talks.
= 工会表示愿意重回谈判桌，递出了橄榄枝。
olive branch = 橄榄枝、和解的表示
hold out an olive branch = 递出橄榄枝
⑦ The trucks were painted olive drab and parked under the trees.
= 那些卡车漆成军绿色，停在树下。
olive drab = 军绿色

Olympiad
/əˈlɪmpiæd/
核心：两支，可数，首字母大写。一是一届奥运会：正式名称里用，前面加序数；二是某些国际性的学科、棋类竞赛：the Chess Olympiad。
① The thirtieth Olympiad was held in London in the summer of 2012.
= 第三十届奥运会于二〇一二年夏天在伦敦举行。
the thirtieth Olympiad = 第三十届奥运会
② She won a silver medal at the International Mathematical Olympiad at sixteen.
= 她十六岁在国际数学奥林匹克竞赛上拿了银牌。
the Mathematical Olympiad = 数学奥林匹克竞赛

Olympian
/əˈlɪmpiən/
核心：两支，首字母大写。一是名词，奥运选手，可数；二是形容词，像神一样超然的、高高在上的：出自希腊众神居住的奥林匹斯山；
也指极大的、非凡的：an Olympian effort。
① The school was opened by an Olympian who had learned to swim in its pool.
= 给学校剪彩的是一位奥运选手，她就是在这所学校的泳池里学会游泳的。
an Olympian = 奥运选手
② The professor watched the argument with Olympian detachment.
= 那位教授带着一种居高临下的超然，旁观那场争论。
Olympian detachment = 居高临下的超然
③ It took an Olympian effort to clear the hall before midnight.
= 费了九牛二虎之力，才在午夜前把大厅清理干净。
an Olympian effort = 九牛二虎之力

Olympic
/əˈlɪmpɪk/
核心：奥运会的、奥林匹克的。形容词，只作定语，首字母大写。
the Olympic Games 是奥运会，也说 the Olympics，动词用复数；the Winter Olympics 是冬奥会。
① She won an Olympic gold medal in the high jump at the age of nineteen.
= 她十九岁拿下了跳高的奥运金牌。
an Olympic gold medal = 奥运金牌
② The Olympic Games are held every four years in a different city.
= 奥运会每四年在不同的城市举行一次。
the Olympic Games = 奥运会
③ He hopes to swim for his country at the next Olympics.
= 他希望在下一届奥运会上代表国家参加游泳比赛。
the Olympics = 奥运会
④ The Olympic flame was carried through the town by a local teacher.
= 奥运圣火由当地的一位教师传递着穿过了镇子。
the Olympic flame = 奥运圣火

Omani
/əʊˈmɑːni/
核心：两支，首字母大写。一是形容词，阿曼的；二是名词，阿曼人，可数。
① The Omani coast has been a centre of the frankincense trade for thousands of years.
= 阿曼的海岸几千年来一直是乳香贸易的中心。
the Omani coast = 阿曼海岸
② Many Omanis work in the oil industry or for the government.
= 许多阿曼人在石油业或政府部门工作。
an Omani = 阿曼人

ombudsman
/ˈɒmbʊdzmən/
核心：申诉专员、监察专员：受理并调查公众对政府部门、银行、公用事业等机构投诉的独立官员。可数，来自瑞典语，复数 ombudsmen。
① She complained to the ombudsman when the bank refused to refund the charge.
= 银行拒绝退还那笔费用，她就向申诉专员投诉。
complain to the ombudsman = 向申诉专员投诉
② The ombudsman found that the council had lost the file and ordered it to pay compensation.
= 申诉专员认定市政会弄丢了档案，责令其赔偿。
the ombudsman = 申诉专员

omega
/ˈəʊmɪɡə/
核心：希腊字母表的最后一个字母，大写 Ω，小写 ω。可数。因为排在最末，引申指终结：the alpha and omega 是始与终、全部要义。
营养学上的 Omega-3 指一类不饱和脂肪酸，鱼油里多，也写作 omega-3。并入本条。
① The symbol for the ohm is the capital letter omega.
= 欧姆的符号是大写的希腊字母 Ω。
the letter omega = 希腊字母 Ω
② For that coach, fitness is the alpha and omega of the game.
= 在那位教练看来，体能是这项运动的全部要义。
the alpha and omega = 始与终、全部要义
③ Oily fish such as sardines are a good source of Omega-3.
= 沙丁鱼这类油性鱼是欧米伽 3 脂肪酸的好来源。
Omega-3 = 欧米伽 3 脂肪酸

omelette
/ˈɒmlət/
核心：煎蛋卷、奄列：把蛋液搅匀后煎成的一张饼，常包上奶酪、火腿、蘑菇再对折。可数，美式拼作 omelet。
谚语 you cannot make an omelette without breaking eggs 是「不打破鸡蛋做不成蛋卷」：要成事总得付出点代价。
① He made a cheese omelette and ate it straight from the pan.
= 他做了个奶酪煎蛋卷，直接就着锅吃了。
a cheese omelette = 奶酪煎蛋卷
② A plain omelette takes three eggs and about two minutes.
= 一个原味煎蛋卷要三个鸡蛋，大约两分钟。
a plain omelette = 原味煎蛋卷
③ The minister defended the job cuts by saying that you cannot make an omelette without breaking eggs.
= 那位大臣为裁员辩护，说不打破鸡蛋做不成蛋卷。
you cannot make an omelette without breaking eggs = 要成事总得付出代价

omen
/ˈəʊmən/
核心：预兆、征兆：被看作预示将来吉凶的事，介词用 of、for。可数。常搭 a good omen、a bad omen、an ill omen。
① The sailors took the calm sea as a good omen for the voyage.
= 水手们把风平浪静看作这趟航行的吉兆。
a good omen = 吉兆
② A broken mirror is thought by some people to be an omen of bad luck.
= 有些人认为，镜子打碎是倒霉的预兆。
an omen of something = 某事的预兆
③ The early frost was a bad omen for the grape harvest.
= 早来的霜对葡萄的收成是个凶兆。
a bad omen for something = 对某事的凶兆

omicron
/əʊˈmaɪkrɒn/
核心：希腊字母表的第十五个字母，大写 Ο，小写 ο，相当于短音的 o。可数。二〇二一年起也因新冠病毒的一个变异株以它命名而广为人知。
① The letter omicron looks exactly like a Roman O.
= 这个希腊字母看上去跟拉丁字母 O 一模一样。
the letter omicron = 希腊字母 ο
② The Omicron variant spread faster than any earlier form of the virus.
= 奥密克戎变异株比那种病毒此前的任何一种形态都传播得快。
the Omicron variant = 奥密克戎变异株

ominous
/ˈɒmɪnəs/
核心：不祥的、预示要出事的：让人觉得有坏事要来。形容词。常搭 an ominous silence、ominous clouds。
副词 ominously 并入本条。
① There was an ominous silence at the other end of the line.
= 电话那头是一阵不祥的沉默。
an ominous silence = 不祥的沉默
② Ominous black clouds were gathering over the hills to the west.
= 西边的山丘上空，不祥的乌云正在聚集。
ominous clouds = 不祥的乌云
③ The engine coughed ominously and then stopped altogether.
= 发动机不祥地咳了几声，然后彻底熄了火。
ominously = 不祥地

omit
/əˈmɪt/
核心：两支，正式。一是省略、删去、漏掉：介词用 from，可以是故意，也可以是疏忽；二是 omit to do something，没有做、忘了做。
过去式 omitted，t 要双写。名词 omission 指省略、遗漏，也指漏掉的东西，并入本条。
① Her name was omitted from the list of prize winners by mistake.
= 她的名字被误从获奖名单上漏掉了。
omit = 省略
be omitted from something = 被从某处漏掉
② You may omit the second paragraph if the letter is too long.
= 信要是太长，第二段可以删去。
omit something = 删去某物
③ He omitted to mention that the car had been in an accident.
= 他没提那辆车出过事故。
omit to do something = 没有做某事
④ The omission of the date makes the document useless in court.
= 文件上漏了日期，拿到法庭上就没用了。
omission = 遗漏
the omission of something = 漏掉某物
⑤ There are several serious omissions in the report.
= 那份报告有好几处严重的遗漏。
an omission = 一处遗漏

omnibus
/ˈɒmnɪbəs/
核心：三支，可数。一是（英式）把一周里播过的几集连起来重播的节目：the omnibus edition；
二是合订本、选集：把一位作者的几部作品合印成一册；三是（旧）公共汽车，bus 就是它的截短。
作定语指包罗多项的：an omnibus bill 是综合法案。
① She missed the weekday episodes and watched the omnibus on Sunday.
= 她没赶上平日播的那几集，就看了星期天的连播。
the omnibus = 一周连播
② He bought an omnibus of four detective novels for the holiday.
= 他买了一本收四部侦探小说的合订本，留着假期看。
an omnibus = 合订本
③ In the old photograph a horse-drawn omnibus is crossing the bridge.
= 在那张旧照片上，一辆马拉的公共马车正在过桥。
a horse-drawn omnibus = 公共马车
④ The changes were hidden in an omnibus bill of four hundred pages.
= 那些改动藏在一份四百页的综合法案里。
an omnibus bill = 综合法案

omnidirectional
/ˌɒmnidəˈrekʃənəl/
核心：全向的：向各个方向发射或从各个方向接收信号的。形容词，技术用语，说天线、话筒、扬声器。
① An omnidirectional microphone in the middle of the table picks up every voice.
= 桌子中央的一只全向话筒，把每个人的声音都收了进去。
an omnidirectional microphone = 全向话筒
② The boat carries an omnidirectional aerial at the top of the mast.
= 那条船的桅杆顶上装着一根全向天线。
an omnidirectional aerial = 全向天线

omnipotent
/ɒmˈnɪpətənt/
核心：全能的、无所不能的、权力无限的。形容词，正式，多说神、君主、国家。名词 omnipotence 并入本条。
① The poem asks why an omnipotent God allows the innocent to suffer.
= 那首诗追问，全能的上帝为什么容许无辜的人受苦。
an omnipotent God = 全能的上帝
② The secretary seemed omnipotent, but she could not make the trains run.
= 那位秘书看上去无所不能，可她没法让火车开动。
seem omnipotent = 看上去无所不能
③ The dictator's omnipotence ended on the day the army refused to fire.
= 军队拒绝开枪的那一天，那个独裁者的无上权力就到了头。
omnipotence = 全能、无上权力

omnipresent
/ˌɒmnɪˈprezənt/
核心：无所不在的、随处可见的。形容词，正式。名词 omnipresence 并入本条。
① The omnipresent cameras record every car that enters the city.
= 无处不在的摄像头记录着每一辆进城的车。
omnipresent cameras = 无处不在的摄像头
② In that country the portrait of the president is omnipresent.
= 在那个国家，总统的画像随处可见。
be omnipresent = 随处可见
③ Visitors remark on the omnipresence of bicycles in the old town.
= 来客都会说起，老城里到处是自行车。
the omnipresence of something = 某物的无所不在

omniscient
/ɒmˈnɪsiənt/
核心：全知的、无所不知的。形容词，正式。文学评论里 an omniscient narrator 是全知叙述者：知道每个人物心里想什么的叙述者。
名词 omniscience 并入本条。
① The novel is told by an omniscient narrator who sees into every character's mind.
= 那部小说由一个全知叙述者讲述，他看得透每个人物的心思。
an omniscient narrator = 全知叙述者
② Doctors are not omniscient, and a second opinion is never an insult.
= 医生不是无所不知的，再找一位医生看看算不上冒犯。
be omniscient = 无所不知
③ Nobody expects omniscience from a weather forecaster.
= 没人指望天气预报员无所不知。
omniscience = 全知

omnishambles
/ˈɒmniʃæmbəlz/
核心：一塌糊涂的局面：处处出错、乱成一锅粥，尤指政府、机构办事。单数，英式口语，出自二〇〇九年的一部政治讽刺剧。
① The launch of the new timetable was an omnishambles from the first morning.
= 新时刻表从启用的头一个早上起就一塌糊涂。
an omnishambles = 一塌糊涂的局面
② The papers called the budget an omnishambles after four reversals in a week.
= 一个星期里四次改口之后，各报把那份预算案称作一场全面的混乱。
call something an omnishambles = 把某事称作全面的混乱

omnivore
/ˈɒmnɪvɔː(r)/
核心：杂食动物：既吃植物也吃肉的动物；也用来说荤素都吃的人，跟 vegetarian 相对。可数。
形容词 omnivorous 是杂食的；引申为（读书、兴趣）来者不拒、什么都涉猎的。并入本条。
① The bear is an omnivore that eats berries, fish, roots and honey.
= 熊是杂食动物，浆果、鱼、根茎、蜂蜜都吃。
an omnivore = 杂食动物
② The café caters for vegans and omnivores at the same table.
= 那家咖啡馆让纯素食者和荤素都吃的人同桌用餐。
omnivores = 荤素都吃的人
③ Pigs are omnivorous and will eat almost anything they are given.
= 猪是杂食的，给什么几乎都吃。
omnivorous = 杂食的
④ She is an omnivorous reader who goes from cookery books to philosophy in a week.
= 她读书来者不拒，一个星期里能从菜谱读到哲学。
an omnivorous reader = 什么书都读的人

on
/ɒn/
核心：介词兼副词，底子是「接触着表面、附着在上面」，是 off 的反义。
介词的主要用法：一是在…上面（接触着）：on the table、on the wall、on the ceiling；
二是在某一天、某个日期：on Monday、on 5 May、on my birthday；三是乘坐（公共交通工具）：on the bus、on a plane；
四是通过某种媒介：on television、on the phone、on the internet；五是关于：a book on birds；
六是处于某种状态、正在进行：on fire、on holiday、on sale、on duty、on strike；七是靠…为生、以…为食：live on rice；
八是属于（委员会、团队）：on the committee；九是一…就：on arrival、on hearing the news；
十是身上带着：have no money on me；十一是由…付钱（口语）：the drinks are on me；十二是方位：on the left。
副词的主要用法：一是穿着、戴着：put your coat on；二是继续下去：go on、carry on；三是开着、通着：the lights are on；
四是在上演、在进行：What's on?；五是往前、往后（时间）：from now on、later on。
固定说法：on and on 是没完没了；on and off 是断断续续；be on about 是（英式口语）唠叨的是；it is not on 是（英式口语）这可不行。
以它打头的复合词并入本条：on-off（时断时续的；也指开关）、on-field（赛场上的）、on-screen（银幕上的、屏幕上的）、
on-street（街边的）、on-ramp（美式，高速公路入口匝道）、on-trend（合潮流的）。
① She left the keys on the kitchen table beside the fruit bowl.
= 她把钥匙留在厨房桌上，果盘旁边。
on the table = 在桌上
② There is a spider on the ceiling above your head.
= 你头顶的天花板上有只蜘蛛。
on the ceiling = 在天花板上
只要接触着表面就用它，不一定在上方。
③ The shop is closed on Sundays and on the first of January.
= 那家店星期天和元旦不开门。
on Sundays = 每逢星期天
④ I read most of the book on the train to Manchester.
= 那本书我大半是在去曼彻斯特的火车上读的。
on the train = 在火车上
⑤ I heard the news on the radio while I was driving home.
= 我是开车回家的时候从收音机里听到那条消息的。
on the radio = 在收音机里
on the phone = 在电话里
⑥ She has written three books on the history of the railway.
= 她写过三本讲铁路史的书。
a book on something = 讲某事的书
⑦ The kitchen was on fire by the time the neighbours noticed the smoke.
= 等邻居看见烟的时候，厨房已经着火了。
on fire = 着火
⑧ The whole family is on holiday in Wales until the end of the month.
= 全家人在威尔士度假，月底才回来。
on holiday = 在度假
on duty = 在值班
⑨ The drivers have been on strike for a week over pay.
= 司机们为了工资已经罢工一个星期。
on strike = 在罢工
⑩ The islanders live mainly on fish, rice and coconuts.
= 岛民主要靠鱼、米饭和椰子过活。
live on something = 靠某物过活
⑪ She has served on the committee for eleven years.
= 她在那个委员会里干了十一年。
on the committee = 在委员会里
⑫ Visitors must sign the book on arrival at the front desk.
= 来访者一到前台就必须登记签名。
on arrival = 一到就
on doing something = 一做某事就
⑬ I have no change on me, so could you pay for the parking?
= 我身上没带零钱，你能付一下停车费吗？
have something on you = 身上带着某物
⑭ Put your wallet away, because lunch is on me today.
= 把钱包收起来，今天午饭我请。
be on somebody = 由某人付钱
⑮ The post office is on the left, just after the bridge.
= 邮局在左手边，刚过桥就是。
on the left = 在左边
⑯ Put your coat on, because it is freezing outside.
= 把大衣穿上，外面冷得要命。
put something on = 穿上某物
这里是副词。
⑰ He went on talking long after everyone had stopped listening.
= 大家早就不听了，他还在往下说。
go on doing something = 继续做某事
⑱ Somebody has left the lights on in the garage.
= 有人没关车库的灯。
leave the lights on = 没关灯
⑲ What is on at the cinema this weekend?
= 这个周末电影院上演什么？
What is on? = 上演什么？
⑳ From now on, the gate will be locked at eight every evening.
= 从现在起，大门每晚八点上锁。
from now on = 从现在起
㉑ We can talk about the money later on, when the children are in bed.
= 钱的事晚些时候再说，等孩子们上了床。
later on = 晚些时候
㉒ The speech went on and on until half the audience had left.
= 那篇讲话没完没了，一直讲到走了一半听众。
on and on = 没完没了
㉓ It has been raining on and off since Tuesday.
= 从星期二起，雨一直断断续续地下。
on and off = 断断续续
㉔ I have no idea what he is on about half the time.
= 他唠叨些什么，我多半听不明白。
be on about = 唠叨的是
㉕ You cannot leave the bill to her again, because it is just not on.
= 你不能又把账单留给她，这可不行。
it is not on = 这可不行
英式口语，说某种做法不像话。
㉖ They have had an on-off friendship for twenty years.
= 他们时好时坏地做了二十年朋友。
on-off = 时断时续的
㉗ The captain apologised for his on-field behaviour after the match.
= 赛后，队长为自己在场上的举止道了歉。
on-field = 赛场上的
㉘ The two stars have a real on-screen chemistry that carries the film.
= 那两位明星在银幕上很来电，撑起了整部片子。
on-screen = 银幕上的
㉙ There is free on-street parking after six in the evening.
= 晚上六点以后街边可以免费停车。
on-street = 街边的
㉚ Traffic was backed up for a mile at the on-ramp to the bridge.
= 上桥的入口匝道那儿，车堵了一英里。
an on-ramp = 入口匝道
㉛ The shop sells cheap copies of whatever is on-trend this month.
= 这个月流行什么，那家店就卖什么的廉价仿品。
on-trend = 合潮流的

onanism
/ˈəʊnənɪzəm/
核心：两支，正式、书面，不可数。一是手淫；二是性交中断。出自《圣经》人物俄南的故事，带旧时道德训诫的口气，如今少用。
① Victorian doctors blamed onanism for everything from weak eyes to madness.
= 维多利亚时代的医生把从视力差到发疯的一切毛病都归咎于手淫。
onanism = 手淫
② The pamphlet of 1760 warned young men against the dangers of onanism.
= 一七六〇年的那本小册子警告年轻人当心手淫的危害。
the dangers of onanism = 手淫的危害

onboard
/ˈɒnbɔːd/
核心：形容词，装在车、船、飞机上的、随机随车的，只作定语：an onboard computer；作副词时多分开写成 on board。
动词是（商业用语）让新员工、新客户入职上手，名词 onboarding 并入本条。
① The car's onboard computer warns the driver when a tyre is losing pressure.
= 那辆车的车载电脑会在轮胎漏气时提醒司机。
an onboard computer = 车载电脑
② The ferry has an onboard shop that opens once the ship leaves the harbour.
= 那艘渡轮上有个小卖部，船一离港就开门。
an onboard shop = 船上的小卖部
③ The firm takes two weeks to onboard each new member of staff.
= 那家公司让每位新员工入职上手要花两个星期。
onboard somebody = 让某人入职上手
④ Her onboarding consisted of a password and a map of the building.
= 她的入职培训就是一个密码和一张大楼平面图。
onboarding = 入职培训

once
/wʌns/
核心：副词两支。一是一次：once a week、once or twice；二是曾经、从前：she once lived here。
连词：一旦、一…就：Once you have signed, you cannot change your mind。
名词：the once、this once 指「这一回」：just this once、for once。
固定说法很多：at once 一是立刻，二是同时；all at once 一是突然，二是一下子全都；
once again 与 once more 是再一次；once in a while 是偶尔；once in a blue moon 是千载难逢；
once and for all 是一劳永逸地；once upon a time 是（童话开头）从前；
谚语 once bitten, twice shy 是一朝被蛇咬，十年怕井绳。
英式口语 once-over 指粗略地看一眼、草草查一遍，并入本条。
① The window cleaner comes once a month and leaves the bill in the letterbox.
= 擦窗工每月来一次，把账单留在信箱里。
once a month = 每月一次
once a week = 每周一次
② I have only met her once, at a wedding in the summer.
= 我只见过她一次，是在夏天的一场婚礼上。
once = 一次
③ She has been late once or twice, but never without ringing.
= 她迟到过一两回，不过从没不打招呼。
once or twice = 一两回
④ This quiet street was once the busiest market in the city.
= 这条僻静的街，曾经是城里最热闹的集市。
once = 曾经
⑤ Once you have tried fresh pasta, you will never buy the dried kind again.
= 你一旦尝过新鲜面条，就再也不会买干面了。
once = 一旦
这里是连词。
⑥ Come here at once, and bring the first-aid box with you.
= 马上过来，把急救箱带上。
at once = 立刻
⑦ Do not all talk at once, or I shall hear nobody.
= 别大家同时说，要不然我谁的话也听不清。
at once = 同时
⑧ All at once the lights went out and the music stopped.
= 突然，灯灭了，音乐也停了。
all at once = 突然
⑨ The train was late once again, for the fourth time that week.
= 火车又晚点了，那个星期已是第四回。
once again = 再一次
⑩ Let us go through the plan once more before the others arrive.
= 趁别人还没到，咱们把计划再过一遍。
once more = 再一次
⑪ We still go to the cinema once in a while, when a good film comes.
= 有好片子的时候，我们偶尔还去电影院。
once in a while = 偶尔
⑫ He rings his mother once in a blue moon and wonders why she is cross.
= 他难得给母亲打一回电话，还纳闷她为什么不高兴。
once in a blue moon = 千载难逢、极少
⑬ The court's ruling settled the question once and for all.
= 法院的裁决一劳永逸地了结了那个问题。
once and for all = 一劳永逸地
⑭ Once upon a time there was a miller who had three sons.
= 从前，有个磨坊主，他有三个儿子。
once upon a time = 从前
⑮ You may stay up late just this once, because it is your birthday.
= 就这一回，你可以晚睡，因为今天是你的生日。
just this once = 就这一回
⑯ For once, the bus arrived early and I had time for a coffee.
= 公共汽车破天荒地早到了，我还有时间喝杯咖啡。
for once = 破天荒地、难得一回
⑰ He lost money on shares in his twenties and has kept it in the bank ever since: once bitten, twice shy.
= 他二十多岁炒股亏了钱，从此把钱一直存在银行里：一朝被蛇咬，十年怕井绳。
once bitten, twice shy = 一朝被蛇咬，十年怕井绳
⑱ The mechanic gave the engine a quick once-over and found nothing wrong.
= 修车工把发动机草草查了一遍，没发现毛病。
a once-over = 粗略的检查

oncology
/ɒŋˈkɒlədʒi/
核心：肿瘤学、肿瘤科：研究和治疗肿瘤、癌症的医学分支。不可数。肿瘤科医生叫 oncologist，并入本条。
① She was referred to the oncology department after the scan.
= 扫描之后，她被转到了肿瘤科。
the oncology department = 肿瘤科
② The oncologist explained the choice between surgery and six weeks of treatment.
= 肿瘤科医生讲解了手术与六周治疗这两种选择。
an oncologist = 肿瘤科医生

oncoming
/ˈɒnkʌmɪŋ/
核心：迎面而来的、即将来临的。形容词，只作定语：oncoming traffic 是对面来的车。
① He swerved to avoid a dog and almost hit an oncoming lorry.
= 他为了躲一条狗猛打方向盘，差点撞上迎面开来的卡车。
an oncoming lorry = 迎面开来的卡车
② Dip your headlights for oncoming traffic on country roads.
= 在乡间公路上，遇到对面来车要把大灯调成近光。
oncoming traffic = 对面来的车
③ The squirrels were busy storing nuts for the oncoming winter.
= 松鼠们忙着为即将到来的冬天储存坚果。
the oncoming winter = 即将到来的冬天

one
/wʌn/
核心：数词、限定词、代词都用。读音跟 won 相同。
数词：一；也指一点钟、一岁。作限定词时比 a 更强调「只一个」：one thing is certain。
跟时间词连用指不确定的某一个：one day（将来或过去的某一天）、one morning。
代词用法：一是替代前面提过的可数名词，免得重复：the red one、which one、the ones on the left；
二是 one of 加复数名词：one of my friends；三是（正式）泛指任何人、包括说话人自己：One should always check，
所有格 one's，反身形式 oneself；口语里多用 you。
成对的说法：one … the other、one … another；one another 是互相；one after another 与 one by one 是一个接一个。
习语：one or two 是一两个、几个；for one thing 是首先、一来；the one and only 是独一无二的；
one and the same 是同一个；at one with 是与…一致、融为一体；one up on 是比…略胜一筹，这种处处占上风的做派叫 one-upmanship；
be a one for 是（英式口语）特别喜欢；a one-off 是（英式）只此一次的事、独一无二的东西。
以它打头的复合词并入本条：one-on-one 与 one-to-one（一对一的）、one-sided（一边倒的、片面的）、one-way（单行的、单程的；
one-way mirror 是单面镜）、one-time（从前的、一次性的）、one-stop（一站式的）、one-piece（连体的）、
one-dimensional（一维的、扁平单薄的）、one-star（一星级的）、one-shot（美式，只此一次的）、one-touch（一键的；足球里一脚出球的）、
one-man 与 one-woman（一个人撑起来的：a one-man band 是一人乐队，引申为单枪匹马干的人）、one-liner（一句话的俏皮话）、
one-night stand（一夜情；也指只演一场的演出）、one-parent family（单亲家庭）、one-track mind（一根筋、满脑子只想一件事）、
one-size-fits-all（一刀切的）、one-trick pony（只会一招的人）、one-day cricket（一天之内打完的板球赛）。
① There is only one biscuit left in the tin.
= 罐子里只剩一块饼干了。
one biscuit = 一块饼干
② The meeting starts at one, so we have time for a sandwich.
= 会一点开，我们来得及吃个三明治。
at one = 在一点钟
③ One thing is certain: the bridge will be closed all summer.
= 有一点是肯定的：那座桥整个夏天都要封闭。
one thing is certain = 有一点是肯定的
④ One day I shall learn to sail, but this is not the year for it.
= 总有一天我要学帆船，不过今年还不是时候。
one day = 总有一天、有一天
⑤ One morning in March a letter arrived that changed everything.
= 三月里的一个早晨，来了一封信，一切都变了。
one morning = 一天早晨
⑥ I prefer the blue shirt to the red one.
= 比起那件红衬衫，我更喜欢蓝的。
the red one = 红的那个
替代前面提过的名词，免得重复。
⑦ "Which seats are ours?" "The ones by the window."
= 「哪些座位是我们的？」「靠窗的那几个。」
the ones = 那几个
which one = 哪一个
⑧ One of my colleagues has just had twins.
= 我有个同事刚生了双胞胎。
one of somebody = 某些人里的一个
⑨ One should always read a contract before signing it.
= 签合同之前，人总该先读一遍。
One should… = 人应该…
很正式；口语里说 you。
⑩ One must look after one's health, as my grandmother used to say.
= 我奶奶常说，人得照顾好自己的身体。
one's = 自己的
⑪ She has two brothers: one is a farmer and the other teaches music.
= 她有两个哥哥，一个务农，另一个教音乐。
one … the other = 一个…另一个
⑫ The neighbours help one another with the harvest every autumn.
= 每年秋天，邻居们互相帮着收庄稼。
one another = 互相
⑬ The guests arrived one after another until the hall was full.
= 客人一个接一个地到，直到大厅坐满。
one after another = 一个接一个
⑭ The children came up one by one to collect their prizes.
= 孩子们一个一个走上来领奖。
one by one = 一个一个地
⑮ I have one or two questions before we sign.
= 签字之前，我有几个问题。
one or two = 一两个、几个
⑯ I cannot come: for one thing, I have no way of getting there.
= 我去不了：首先，我没法到那儿。
for one thing = 首先、一来
⑰ Please welcome the one and only Ella Marsh.
= 让我们欢迎独一无二的埃拉·马什。
the one and only = 独一无二的
⑱ It turned out that the caller and the thief were one and the same person.
= 原来打电话的人和那个贼是同一个人。
one and the same = 同一个
⑲ On this point the minister is at one with her critics.
= 在这一点上，那位大臣跟批评她的人意见一致。
at one with = 与…一致
⑳ He always has to be one up on his brother, whether in cars or holidays.
= 不管是车还是度假，他总得比弟弟略胜一筹。
one up on = 比…略胜一筹
㉑ Their dinner parties are exercises in one-upmanship.
= 他们的晚宴是处处要压人一头的比拼。
one-upmanship = 处处占上风的做派
㉒ She is a great one for lists and writes three before breakfast.
= 她特别爱列清单，早饭前就写三张。
be a one for something = 特别喜欢某事
㉓ The concert is a one-off and will not be repeated.
= 那场音乐会只此一场，不会再演。
a one-off = 只此一次的事
㉔ Each pupil has a one-to-one lesson with the teacher every week.
= 每个学生每周跟老师上一节一对一的课。
one-to-one = 一对一的
one-on-one = 一对一的
㉕ The match was completely one-sided and finished seven nil.
= 那场比赛完全一边倒，以七比零收场。
one-sided = 一边倒的、片面的
㉖ The street is one-way, so you will have to drive round the block.
= 那条街是单行道，你得绕着街区开一圈。
one-way = 单行的、单程的
㉗ The detectives watched the interview through a one-way mirror.
= 探员们透过单面镜观看问讯。
a one-way mirror = 单面镜
㉘ The hall is run by a one-time boxer who now teaches dancing.
= 管那座礼堂的是个当年的拳击手，如今教跳舞。
one-time = 从前的
㉙ The council has opened a one-stop shop for advice on housing and benefits.
= 市政会开了一个一站式的窗口，提供住房和补助方面的咨询。
one-stop = 一站式的
㉚ She packed a one-piece swimsuit and a pair of goggles.
= 她带了一件连体泳衣和一副泳镜。
one-piece = 连体的
㉛ The villain is one-dimensional and has no reason for anything he does.
= 那个反派扁平单薄，做什么都没有缘由。
one-dimensional = 扁平单薄的
㉜ We stayed in a one-star hotel with a shared bathroom.
= 我们住的是一家一星级旅馆，浴室共用。
one-star = 一星级的
㉝ The grant is a one-shot payment and will not be renewed.
= 那笔拨款只发一次，不会续发。
one-shot = 只此一次的
㉞ The team's quick one-touch passing wore the defenders down.
= 那支球队快速的一脚出球把对方后卫拖垮了。
one-touch = 一脚出球的、一键的
㉟ He runs the shop as a one-man band and has not had a holiday in years.
= 那家店他一个人撑着，好多年没休过假了。
a one-man band = 单枪匹马干的人
one-man = 一个人的
㊱ She tours with a one-woman show about her years as a nurse.
= 她带着一出讲自己护士生涯的独角戏四处巡演。
one-woman = 一个女人撑起来的
㊲ The comedian is famous for one-liners that take a second to sink in.
= 那位喜剧演员以要愣一下才听懂的一句话俏皮话出名。
a one-liner = 一句话的俏皮话
㊳ The band played a one-night stand in every town along the coast.
= 那支乐队在沿海的每座城镇各演一场。
a one-night stand = 只演一场的演出、一夜情
㊴ The scheme offers free school meals to children from one-parent families.
= 那项计划给单亲家庭的孩子提供免费校餐。
a one-parent family = 单亲家庭
㊵ He has a one-track mind and can talk about nothing but fishing.
= 他一根筋，除了钓鱼什么都不会聊。
a one-track mind = 一根筋
㊶ A one-size-fits-all policy will not work for schools as different as these.
= 对差别这么大的学校，一刀切的政策行不通。
one-size-fits-all = 一刀切的
㊷ Critics called the striker a one-trick pony who could only shoot from distance.
= 评论说那名前锋只会一招，就是远射。
a one-trick pony = 只会一招的人
㊸ One-day cricket brought new crowds to a game that used to last five days.
= 一日制板球给这项从前要打五天的运动带来了新观众。
one-day cricket = 一日制板球

oneness
/ˈwʌnnəs/
核心：一体、合一、融为一体的感觉：介词用 with。不可数，书面。
① Walking alone on the moor gave her a sense of oneness with the land.
= 独自走在荒原上，她有一种与大地融为一体的感觉。
a sense of oneness with something = 与某物融为一体的感觉
② The preacher spoke of the oneness of all people before God.
= 那位牧师讲到，在上帝面前人人同为一体。
the oneness of something = 某事物的一体

onerous
/ˈəʊnərəs/
核心：繁重的、费力的、成为负担的：多说任务、职责、条件。形容词，正式。
① She took on the onerous task of sorting forty years of family papers.
= 她揽下了整理四十年家庭文书的繁重差事。
an onerous task = 繁重的差事
② The terms of the lease are onerous and include all repairs to the roof.
= 那份租约的条款很苛刻，连屋顶的一切修缮都算在内。
onerous terms = 苛刻的条款

oneself
/wʌnˈself/
核心：one 的反身代词，正式：泛指「任何人自己」。用法跟 himself、yourself 一样：一是反身，二是强调。
词典里列短语时用它代表各人称的反身代词：enjoy oneself；口语里对应的是 yourself。
by oneself 是独自、靠自己；be oneself 是保持本色、状态正常。
① One has to remind oneself that the guests are tired as well.
= 人得提醒自己，客人们也累了。
remind oneself = 提醒自己
② It is easier to do the job oneself than to explain it to someone else.
= 自己动手干，比讲给别人听省事。
do something oneself = 自己动手做某事
这里是强调用法。
③ Living by oneself teaches one to cook, whether one likes it or not.
= 一个人过日子，不管愿不愿意，总得学会做饭。
by oneself = 独自
④ The secret of a good interview is simply to be oneself.
= 面试成功的秘诀，无非是保持本色。
be oneself = 保持本色

onesie
/ˈwʌnzi/
核心：两支，可数。一是（成人穿的）连体家居服、连体睡衣；二是（美式）婴儿的连体衣。原是商标名。
① He spent the whole of Sunday on the sofa in a tiger onesie.
= 他穿着一件老虎连体睡衣，在沙发上窝了整个星期天。
a onesie = 连体家居服
② Pack three clean onesies for the baby and a spare blanket.
= 给宝宝带三件干净的连体衣，再带一条备用的毯子。
onesies for the baby = 婴儿连体衣

ongoing
/ˈɒnɡəʊɪŋ/
核心：持续的、仍在进行的。形容词，多作定语，说调查、讨论、问题、过程。
① The police refused to comment on an ongoing investigation.
= 警方拒绝就一项仍在进行的调查置评。
an ongoing investigation = 仍在进行的调查
② Damp is an ongoing problem in the houses along the river.
= 在沿河的房子里，潮湿是个老大难问题。
an ongoing problem = 持续存在的问题
③ Training is an ongoing process and does not end with the first week.
= 培训是个持续的过程，不是头一个星期就完事。
an ongoing process = 持续的过程

onion
/ˈʌnjən/
核心：洋葱：一种球形蔬菜，一层层剥开，味道辛辣，切的时候呛眼睛。可数，作食材时也不可数。
常搭 chop an onion、fried onions；spring onion 是（英式）小葱，美式叫 scallion。
英式口语习语 know your onions 是很在行。
onion ring 是炸洋葱圈；onion dome 是（俄罗斯教堂上的）洋葱形圆顶；onion-skin paper 是一种极薄的半透明纸。并入本条。
① Chop an onion finely and fry it gently in butter until it is soft.
= 把一个洋葱切细，用黄油小火煎软。
onion = 洋葱
chop an onion = 切洋葱
② The burger came with fried onions and a slice of cheese.
= 汉堡里夹着煎洋葱和一片奶酪。
fried onions = 煎洋葱
③ Scatter a few chopped spring onions over the soup before serving.
= 上桌前在汤上撒一点切碎的小葱。
spring onion = 小葱
④ Ask the plumber, because he really knows his onions.
= 问那个水管工吧，他是真在行。
know your onions = 很在行
⑤ We shared a plate of onion rings while we waited for the pizza.
= 等比萨的时候，我们合吃了一盘炸洋葱圈。
an onion ring = 炸洋葱圈
⑥ The cathedral has five gold onion domes that shine in the snow.
= 那座大教堂有五个金色的洋葱形圆顶，在雪中闪闪发亮。
an onion dome = 洋葱形圆顶
⑦ The old airmail letters were typed on onion-skin paper to save weight.
= 从前的航空信为了省重量，是打在薄葱皮纸上的。
onion-skin paper = 薄葱皮纸

online
/ˌɒnˈlaɪn/
核心：形容词兼副词：在线的、联网的、通过互联网的：online shopping、go online、buy something online。反义 offline。
come online 还指（设备、工厂）投入运转。online dating 是网上交友、网恋。
① She does most of her food shopping online and has it delivered.
= 她买菜大多在网上买，让人送货上门。
shop online = 网购
② The form can be filled in online in about ten minutes.
= 那张表可以在线填写，大约十分钟。
fill something in online = 在线填写某物
③ The college offers an online course for people who cannot attend in the day.
= 那所学院给白天来不了的人开了一门网课。
an online course = 网课
④ The new power station will come online at the end of next year.
= 新电站将在明年年底投入运转。
come online = 投入运转
⑤ They met through online dating and married within a year.
= 他们是通过网上交友认识的，不到一年就结了婚。
online dating = 网上交友

onlooker
/ˈɒnlʊkə(r)/
核心：旁观者、围观的人：在一旁看着事情发生而不参与的人。可数。
① A crowd of onlookers had gathered behind the police tape.
= 警戒线后面聚了一群围观的人。
a crowd of onlookers = 一群围观的人
② One onlooker filmed the rescue on her phone and sent it to the news.
= 有个旁观者用手机拍下了营救过程，发给了新闻台。
an onlooker = 旁观者

only
/ˈəʊnli/
核心：形容词、副词、连词都用。
形容词：唯一的、仅有的，只作定语：the only one、an only child（独生子女）；the only thing is 用来引出一点保留意见。
副词：只、仅仅、才：位置通常在实义动词前，口语里靠重音指明限定哪一部分，书面里最好紧挨着被限定的词。
放在句首修饰状语时后面倒装：Only then did I understand。
only just 一是刚刚，二是勉强、险些没；only too 加形容词是非常、巴不得；
only to do something 表示出乎意料、让人失望的结果；if only 表示但愿、要是…就好了；not only … but also 是不但…而且。
连词（口语）：只是、不过：I would come, only I have no car。
① This is the only photograph that we have of my grandfather as a boy.
= 这是我们手里唯一一张我祖父小时候的照片。
the only photograph = 唯一的一张照片
② She is an only child and was brought up among adults.
= 她是独生女，在大人堆里长大。
an only child = 独生子女
③ The flat is lovely; the only thing is that it is on the fifth floor.
= 那套公寓很好，只是它在六楼。
the only thing is = 只是、唯一的问题是
④ I only wanted to help, and now nobody is speaking to me.
= 我只是想帮忙，现在倒没人跟我说话了。
only = 只
⑤ Only members may use the car park at the back.
= 只有会员才可以用后面的停车场。
only members = 只有会员
紧挨着被限定的词，意思最清楚。
⑥ The bridge was finished only last year, and it already needs repairs.
= 那座桥去年才完工，现在已经要修了。
only last year = 去年才
⑦ Only then did I realise that I had left my passport at home.
= 直到那时我才发觉，护照落在家里了。
Only then did I… = 直到那时我才…
放在句首，后面倒装。
⑧ We have only just moved in, so the boxes are still in the hall.
= 我们刚搬进来，箱子还堆在门厅里。
only just = 刚刚
⑨ She only just caught the last train, with ten seconds to spare.
= 她险些没赶上末班车，只剩十秒钟。
only just = 勉强、险些没
⑩ I shall be only too pleased to show you round the garden.
= 我非常乐意带你们在花园里转转。
only too = 非常、巴不得
⑪ He hurried to the station, only to find that the train had been cancelled.
= 他急匆匆赶到车站，却发现那班车取消了。
only to = 结果却
only to find that… = 结果却发现…
⑫ If only I had listened to her, we would not be in this mess.
= 我当初要是听了她的话就好了，我们就不会落到这步田地。
if only = 但愿、要是…就好了
⑬ She not only speaks Welsh but also writes poetry in it.
= 她不但会说威尔士语，还用它写诗。
not only = 不但
⑭ I would lend you the money, only I have none myself.
= 我愿意借钱给你，只是我自己也没有。
only = 只是、不过
这里是连词，口语用法。

onomatopoeia
/ˌɒnəˌmætəˈpiːə/
核心：拟声、象声：用读音模仿实际声响来造词的手法，如 buzz、hiss、cuckoo。不可数，语言学与修辞用语。
形容词 onomatopoeic 并入本条。
① Words like "splash" and "crack" are examples of onomatopoeia.
= 模仿水溅声和断裂声的那类词，是拟声的例子。
onomatopoeia = 拟声
② The poet uses onomatopoeic verbs to bring the storm into the room.
= 那位诗人用拟声的动词，把暴风雨带进了屋里。
onomatopoeic = 拟声的

onrush
/ˈɒnrʌʃ/
核心：猛冲、奔涌、汹涌而来：水、人群、情感突然而有力地涌上来。单数，正式或文学，介词用 of。
① The wall could not hold back the onrush of water from the hills.
= 那堵墙挡不住从山上奔涌而下的水。
an onrush of water = 奔涌而来的水
② She was surprised by an onrush of tears at the end of the film.
= 影片结尾，她的眼泪突然涌了上来，连自己都吃了一惊。
an onrush of tears = 突然涌上的眼泪

onset
/ˈɒnset/
核心：（不愉快的事的）开始、发作、来临：the onset of winter、the onset of the disease。单数，带 the，介词用 of。
① The roof must be mended before the onset of winter.
= 屋顶必须在冬天来临之前修好。
the onset of winter = 冬天的来临
② The onset of the illness is sudden, with a high fever and a headache.
= 那种病发作很突然，伴有高烧和头痛。
the onset of an illness = 疾病的发作

onshore
/ˌɒnˈʃɔː(r)/
核心：形容词兼副词，两支。一是在陆上的、岸上的：onshore wind farms；二是（风）从海上吹向陆地的。反义 offshore。
① The company is building its first onshore wind farm on the hills above the town.
= 那家公司正在镇子上方的山丘上建它的头一座陆上风电场。
an onshore wind farm = 陆上风电场
② A strong onshore wind drove the waves high up the beach.
= 一阵强劲的向岸风把海浪推上了沙滩高处。
an onshore wind = 向岸风

onside
/ˌɒnˈsaɪd/
核心：两支。一是（足球、橄榄球）不越位的，反义 offside；二是（英式）站在自己这一边的、支持的：get somebody onside。
① The replay showed that the striker was just onside when the ball was played.
= 回放显示，传球的那一刻前锋刚好没有越位。
be onside = 没有越位
② The minister needs to get the unions onside before the vote.
= 那位大臣得在表决前把工会争取到自己这一边。
get somebody onside = 把某人争取过来

onslaught
/ˈɒnslɔːt/
核心：猛攻、猛烈的抨击：介词用 on、against；也指大量涌来、难以招架的东西：an onslaught of。可数。
① The town held out for three weeks against the enemy's onslaught.
= 那座城在敌人的猛攻下坚守了三个星期。
an onslaught = 猛攻
② The minister faced an onslaught of criticism from her own party.
= 那位大臣遭到本党铺天盖地的批评。
an onslaught of criticism = 铺天盖地的批评
③ The little café could not cope with the summer onslaught of tourists.
= 那家小咖啡馆应付不了夏天蜂拥而来的游客。
an onslaught of tourists = 蜂拥而来的游客

onstage
/ˌɒnˈsteɪdʒ/
核心：形容词兼副词：在舞台上的、当着观众的。反义 offstage。
① The two actors hated each other but were the best of friends onstage.
= 那两位演员彼此憎恶，在台上却是最要好的朋友。
onstage = 在台上
② The band walked onstage an hour late to a chorus of boos.
= 乐队晚了一个钟头才上台，迎来一片嘘声。
walk onstage = 走上台

onto
/ˈɒntə/（辅音前）/ˈɒntu/（元音前）
核心：介词。一是到…上面：表示朝着某个表面移动，跟只表示位置的 on 不同；也写作 on to。
二是 be onto somebody，（口语）察觉了某人干的坏事、盯上了某人；be onto something 是摸到了门道、发现了有价值的线索。
三是 get onto somebody，（英式）跟某人联系；get onto a subject 是说到某个话题。
① The cat jumped onto the table and knocked the jug to the floor.
= 那只猫跳上桌子，把水罐碰到了地上。
jump onto something = 跳到某物上
② He stepped onto the platform just as the train began to move.
= 火车刚要开动，他迈上了站台。
step onto something = 迈上某处
表示移动的方向；只说位置时用 on。
③ The police are onto him and have been watching the house for a week.
= 警方已经盯上了他，监视那座房子一个星期了。
be onto somebody = 盯上某人
④ The scientists believe that they are onto something important.
= 那些科学家相信，他们摸到了重要的线索。
be onto something = 摸到了门道
⑤ I shall get onto the council first thing tomorrow about the broken streetlight.
= 我明天一早就跟市政会联系，说路灯坏了的事。
get onto somebody = 跟某人联系
⑥ How did we get onto the subject of school dinners?
= 我们怎么说到学校午餐这个话题上来了？
get onto a subject = 说到某个话题

ontology
/ɒnˈtɒlədʒi/
核心：两支。一是（哲学）本体论：研究「存在」本身的性质的分支，不可数；二是（计算机）本体：对某个领域里的概念及其关系的形式化描述，可数。
形容词 ontological 并入本条。
① The first chapter deals with ontology and asks what it means for a thing to exist.
= 第一章讲本体论，追问一样东西存在是什么意思。
ontology = 本体论
② The team built an ontology of medical terms for the hospital's records.
= 那个团队为医院的病历建了一套医学术语的本体。
an ontology = 一套本体
③ The ontological argument tries to prove the existence of God from the idea of God alone.
= 本体论论证试图单从上帝这个观念出发证明上帝存在。
the ontological argument = 本体论论证

onus
/ˈəʊnəs/
核心：责任、义务、举证的担子：the onus is on somebody to do something。单数，带 the，正式。
① The onus is on the landlord to prove that the damage was caused by the tenant.
= 证明损坏是房客造成的，这个责任在房东。
the onus is on somebody to do something = 做某事的责任在某人
② The new law shifts the onus of proof from the buyer to the seller.
= 新法把举证责任从买方转到了卖方。
the onus of proof = 举证责任

onward
/ˈɒnwəd/
核心：两支。一是形容词，继续向前的，只作定语：the onward journey 是接下来的行程；
二是副词，向前、往后：英式多写成 onwards，美式多用不带 s 的形式；from … onwards 是从…起。
① We had an hour in Paris before the onward flight to Rome.
= 飞往罗马的下一程航班之前，我们在巴黎有一个钟头。
the onward flight = 下一程航班
② The pool is open from six o'clock onwards every weekday.
= 泳池每个工作日从六点起开放。
from six o'clock onwards = 从六点起
onwards = 向前、往后
③ The army marched onward through the night without a halt.
= 军队连夜向前行军，一刻不停。
march onward = 向前行军

onyx
/ˈɒnɪks/
核心：缟玛瑙：一种有平行条纹的宝石，多为黑白相间，用来做首饰和摆件。不可数。
① The ring is set with a square of black onyx.
= 那枚戒指镶着一块方形的黑色缟玛瑙。
black onyx = 黑色缟玛瑙
② A chess set carved from onyx stood on the table by the window.
= 窗边的桌上摆着一副缟玛瑙雕的国际象棋。
carved from onyx = 缟玛瑙雕的

oodles
/ˈuːdəlz/
核心：大量、许许多多：oodles of 加名词。复数名词，口语，带轻松夸张的口气。
① The cake is made with oodles of butter and very little flour.
= 那个蛋糕用了大量的黄油，面粉却很少。
oodles of butter = 大量的黄油
② There is no hurry, because we have oodles of time before the film starts.
= 不用急，离电影开场还有大把的时间。
oodles of time = 大把的时间

ooh
/uː/
核心：感叹词，表示惊喜、赞叹、兴奋，也表示突然的疼痛。动词是发出这样的赞叹声：ooh and aah 是啧啧称奇。
① Ooh, look at the colour of that sky over the harbour.
= 哇，你看港口上空那天色。
Ooh, … = 哇，…
② The crowd oohed and aahed as each firework burst above the river.
= 每一朵烟花在河面上空炸开，人群都发出啧啧的赞叹。
ooh and aah = 啧啧称奇

oompah
/ˈʊmpɑː/
核心：嗡吧吧：铜管乐队里大号一低一高的伴奏声，也指这种节奏的音乐。不可数，口语，常作定语：an oompah band。
① An oompah band in leather shorts played in the square all afternoon.
= 一支穿皮短裤的铜管乐队在广场上吹了一下午。
an oompah band = 铜管乐队
② We could hear the oompah of the tuba from three streets away.
= 隔着三条街，我们都听得见大号嗡吧吧的声音。
the oompah of a tuba = 大号的嗡吧声

oomph
/ʊmf/
核心：劲头、活力、冲劲：让一样东西显得有力量、有吸引力的那股劲。不可数，口语。
① The engine is quiet but has plenty of oomph on the hills.
= 那台发动机很安静，爬坡时却劲头十足。
have plenty of oomph = 劲头十足
② The speech was sensible enough but lacked oomph.
= 那篇讲话倒是合情合理，就是缺了点冲劲。
lack oomph = 缺了点冲劲

oops
/ʊps/
核心：感叹词：哎哟、糟了：自己犯了个小错、出了点小岔子时说，也用来说险些出事。口语。
孩子摔倒时大人哄着说 oops-a-daisy，并入本条。
① Oops, I have just sent that email to the whole company.
= 糟了，我把那封邮件发给全公司了。
Oops, … = 糟了，…
② "Oops!" she said, catching the glass an inch from the floor.
= 「哎哟！」她说着，在离地一英寸的地方接住了杯子。
Oops! = 哎哟！
③ "Oops-a-daisy," said his father, lifting the boy back onto his feet.
= 「哎哟哟，起来喽。」他爸爸说着，把男孩扶了起来。
oops-a-daisy = 哎哟哟（哄摔倒的孩子）

ooze
/uːz/
核心：动词两支。一是（黏稠的液体）慢慢渗出、流出：介词用 from、out of，也可及物；二是（人）浑身散发出某种气质：ooze confidence。
名词是淤泥、软泥，不可数；也指缓慢的渗流。形容词 oozy 是渗着黏液的、泥泞的，并入本条。
① Blood was oozing from a cut above his eye.
= 血正从他眼睛上方的一道口子里渗出来。
ooze from something = 从某处渗出
② The cheese oozed out of the pastry as soon as she cut it.
= 她一切下去，奶酪就从酥皮里流了出来。
ooze out of something = 从某物里流出
③ The new captain oozes confidence, even after three defeats.
= 新队长浑身透着自信，哪怕已经连输三场。
ooze confidence = 浑身透着自信
④ His boots sank into the ooze at the edge of the pond.
= 他的靴子陷进了池塘边的淤泥里。
the ooze = 淤泥
⑤ The children came back from the river with oozy mud up to their knees.
= 孩子们从河边回来，烂泥一直糊到膝盖。
oozy mud = 烂泥

op
/ɒp/
